# Inline runtime chunk 04

# ===== resource_planner_v190.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["resource_planner_v190"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.9.0 ResourceGroup 决策与缺集拆包层。\n"
    "\n"
    "决策原则：\n"
    "1. 先确定订阅当前真正缺什么；\n"
    "2. 同一 Telegram 消息中的光鸭分享 / Magnet / ED2K 是同一 ResourceGroup 的候选；\n"
    "3. 同等满足订阅规则时，光鸭直接转存优先；其正在落盘的剧集会被 reservation 占用；\n"
    "4. 只对剩余未覆盖剧集选择 Magnet，其次 ED2K；\n"
    "5. Magnet resolve 后按高置信集号只选缺集文件，无法可靠识别时不整包误存；\n"
    "6. 已创建 taskId 永远由既有 v1.8.0 安全层轮询，不重复 create_task。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "from .episode_resolver_v190 import AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE, reliable_episode_set, resolve_episode\n"
    "from .legacy import _entry_match_reason, _is_subtitle, _is_video\n"
    "from .source_types_v180 import normalize_ed2k, source_identity\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX = \"EPISODE_AMBIGUOUS:\"\n"
    "_ACTIVE_CLAIM_STATES = {\"new\", \"retry\", \"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _ed2k_uri_basename(uri: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    text = str(uri or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not text.lower().startswith(\"ed2k://|file|\"):\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return str(normalize_ed2k(text).get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        parts = text.split(\"|\")\n"
    "        if len(parts) >= 3:\n"
    "            return str(parts[2] or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaResourcePlannerMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"必须放在 OfflineSafety / MultiSource 之前，让所有来源共用同一决策。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    _episode_auto_confidence = AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE\n"
    "    _channel_external_auto_dispatch = True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            threshold = float(config.get(\"episode_auto_confidence\", AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            threshold = AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE\n"
    "        self._episode_auto_confidence = max(0.80, min(threshold, 1.0))\n"
    "        self._channel_external_auto_dispatch = bool(config.get(\"channel_external_auto_dispatch\", True))\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 来源元数据 / ResourceGroup\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _upsert_source(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe_id: int,\n"
    "        uri: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        label: str = \"\",\n"
    "        origin: str = \"manual\",\n"
    "        enabled: bool = True,\n"
    "        auto_dispatch: Optional[bool] = None,\n"
    "        resource_group_id: str = \"\",\n"
    "        target_episodes: Optional[Iterable[int]] = None,\n"
    "        episode_hint: str = \"\",\n"
    "        source_label: str = \"\",\n"
    "        message_id: str = \"\",\n"
    "        candidate_rank: int = 0,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = super()._upsert_source(\n"
    "            subscribe_id,\n"
    "            uri,\n"
    "            label=label,\n"
    "            origin=origin,\n"
    "            enabled=enabled,\n"
    "            auto_dispatch=auto_dispatch,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        extras: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        if resource_group_id:\n"
    "            extras[\"resource_group_id\"] = str(resource_group_id)[:80]\n"
    "        if target_episodes is not None:\n"
    "            values = []\n"
    "            for raw in target_episodes:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    value = int(raw)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if value > 0 and value not in values:\n"
    "                    values.append(value)\n"
    "            extras[\"target_episodes\"] = sorted(values)\n"
    "        if episode_hint:\n"
    "            extras[\"episode_hint\"] = str(episode_hint)[:120]\n"
    "        if source_label:\n"
    "            extras[\"source_label\"] = str(source_label)[:120]\n"
    "        if message_id:\n"
    "            extras[\"message_id\"] = str(message_id)[:80]\n"
    "        extras[\"candidate_rank\"] = int(candidate_rank or 0)\n"
    "        if extras:\n"
    "            row = self._update_source(str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\"), **extras) or row\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _existing_source(self, subscribe_id: int, source_type: str, identity: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        key = source_identity(str(source_type or \"\"), str(identity or \"\"), sid)\n"
    "        return dict(self._source_store()[\"items\"].get(key) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _active_source_claims(self, subscribe_id: int) -> set[int]:\n"
    "        claims: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        for row in self._source_store()[\"items\"].values():\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict) or int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\") not in _ACTIVE_CLAIM_STATES:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            for raw in row.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or row.get(\"target_episodes\") or []:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    value = int(raw)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if value > 0:\n"
    "                    claims.add(value)\n"
    "        return claims\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _source_descriptor(source: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        return \" \".join(\n"
    "            str(value or \"\")\n"
    "            for value in (source.get(\"name\"), source.get(\"label\"), source.get(\"type\"))\n"
    "            if value\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _external_resource_allowed(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        entry: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n"
    "        # 复用 legacy 的 include/exclude/resolution/quality/effect 规则，保证分享与磁力判断一致。\n"
    "        descriptor_name = self._source_descriptor(source)\n"
    "        probe = {\"files\": [{\"relative_path\": descriptor_name, \"name\": descriptor_name}]}\n"
    "        return self._subscription_resource_allowed(subscribe, entry, probe)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Magnet resolve 后的文件级缺集选择\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _subfiles_by_file_index_v210(resolve_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Map real API fileIndex → subfile row. Never treat list position as fileIndex.\"\"\"\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(bt_info, dict):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") or []\n"
    "        if not isinstance(subfiles, list):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        by_file_index: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for fallback_index, row in enumerate(subfiles):\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            raw_index = row.get(\"fileIndex\")\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                index = int(raw_index) if raw_index is not None else int(fallback_index)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                index = int(fallback_index)\n"
    "            by_file_index[index] = row\n"
    "        return by_file_index\n"
    "\n"
    "    @classmethod\n"
    "    def _selected_manifest_v210(\n"
    "        cls,\n"
    "        resolve_data: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        selected_indexes: List[int],\n"
    "    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        by_file_index = cls._subfiles_by_file_index_v210(resolve_data)\n"
    "        manifest: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for raw in selected_indexes or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                index = int(raw)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row = by_file_index.get(index)\n"
    "            if not row:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            name = str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                size = int(row.get(\"fileSize\") or row.get(\"size\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                size = 0\n"
    "            if _is_video(name):\n"
    "                kind = \"video\"\n"
    "            elif _is_subtitle(name):\n"
    "                kind = \"subtitle\"\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                kind = \"other\"\n"
    "            manifest.append({\n"
    "                \"file_index\": index,\n"
    "                \"name\": name,\n"
    "                \"size\": size,\n"
    "                \"type\": kind,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return manifest\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        media: str,\n"
    "        source_type: str,\n"
    "        resolve_data: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        selected_indexes: List[int],\n"
    "        decision: str,\n"
    "    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        by_file_index = self._subfiles_by_file_index_v210(resolve_data)\n"
    "        total = len(by_file_index)\n"
    "        videos = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in by_file_index.values()\n"
    "            if _is_video(str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        subtitles = sum(\n"
    "            1 for row in by_file_index.values()\n"
    "            if _is_subtitle(str(row.get(\"fileName\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        manifest = self._selected_manifest_v210(resolve_data, selected_indexes)\n"
    "        selected_video = sum(1 for row in manifest if row.get(\"type\") == \"video\")\n"
    "        selected_subtitle = sum(1 for row in manifest if row.get(\"type\") == \"subtitle\")\n"
    "        sample = []\n"
    "        for row in manifest[:12]:\n"
    "            base = str(row.get(\"name\") or \"\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "            sample.append(f\"{row.get('file_index')}:{row.get('type')}:{row.get('size')}:{base[:80]}\")\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【云添加文件选择】media=%s source=%s total=%s videos=%s subtitles=%s \"\n"
    "            \"selected_total=%s selected_video=%s selected_subtitle=%s decision=%s files=%s\",\n"
    "            str(media or \"-\")[:120],\n"
    "            str(source_type or \"-\").upper(),\n"
    "            total,\n"
    "            videos,\n"
    "            subtitles,\n"
    "            len(manifest),\n"
    "            selected_video,\n"
    "            selected_subtitle,\n"
    "            decision,\n"
    "            \";\".join(sample) or \"-\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return manifest\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _assert_selected_video_present_v210(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        resolve_data: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        selected_indexes: List[int],\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Hard gate: never create_task for subtitle-only selections.\"\"\"\n"
    "        source_type = str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\").upper()\n"
    "        media = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or source.get(\"name\") or source.get(\"label\") or \"\")\n"
    "        # ED2K single-file without subfiles: treat resolved name as the only candidate.\n"
    "        by_file_index = self._subfiles_by_file_index_v210(resolve_data)\n"
    "        if not by_file_index and not selected_indexes:\n"
    "            # ED2K / whole-package path with no enumerable indexes: still require video name.\n"
    "            source_type_l = str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "            if source_type_l == \"ed2k\":\n"
    "                probe = str(\n"
    "                    (resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") or {}).get(\"fileName\")\n"
    "                    if isinstance(resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\"), dict)\n"
    "                    else \"\"\n"
    "                ) or str(source.get(\"name\") or source.get(\"label\") or source.get(\"resolved_name\") or \"\")\n"
    "                # Prefer resolved_name from caller via source fields when present.\n"
    "                if not _is_video(probe):\n"
    "                    self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                        media=media,\n"
    "                        source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                        resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                        selected_indexes=[],\n"
    "                        decision=\"reject\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                        f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING: \"\n"
    "                        f\"ED2K 无 subfiles 且文件名不是正片：{probe[:180] or '-'}\"\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "            self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                media=media,\n"
    "                source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                selected_indexes=[],\n"
    "                decision=\"submit_whole_or_ed2k\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not by_file_index and selected_indexes:\n"
    "            # No enumerable subfiles — allow submit only when selection is empty fileIndexes\n"
    "            # (whole package) or ED2K path already validated episodes. Still log.\n"
    "            self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                media=media,\n"
    "                source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                selected_indexes=[],\n"
    "                decision=\"submit_whole_or_ed2k\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "\n"
    "        if selected_indexes and by_file_index:\n"
    "            manifest = self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                media=media,\n"
    "                source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                selected_indexes=selected_indexes,\n"
    "                decision=\"precheck\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            video_count = sum(1 for row in manifest if row.get(\"type\") == \"video\")\n"
    "            if video_count < 1:\n"
    "                self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                    media=media,\n"
    "                    source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                    resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                    selected_indexes=selected_indexes,\n"
    "                    decision=\"reject\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING: \"\n"
    "                    \"最终选中文件仅有字幕/非视频，禁止创建光鸭云添加任务\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            self._log_cloud_file_selection_v210(\n"
    "                media=media,\n"
    "                source_type=source_type,\n"
    "                resolve_data=resolve_data,\n"
    "                selected_indexes=selected_indexes,\n"
    "                decision=\"submit\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return manifest\n"
    "        return []\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _media_rows(resolve_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(bt_info, dict):\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") or []\n"
    "        if not isinstance(subfiles, list):\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        for fallback_index, raw in enumerate(subfiles):\n"
    "            if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            value = raw.get(\"fileIndex\")\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                index = int(value) if value is not None else fallback_index\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                index = fallback_index\n"
    "            name = str(raw.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                size = int(raw.get(\"fileSize\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                size = 0\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"index\": index,\n"
    "                \"name\": name,\n"
    "                \"size\": size,\n"
    "                \"video\": _is_video(name),\n"
    "                \"subtitle\": _is_subtitle(name),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _parent_path(value: str) -> str:\n"
    "        normalized = str(value or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n"
    "        return normalized.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0].lower() if \"/\" in normalized else \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _ed2k_single_file_selection_v190(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        resolve_data: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"ED2K no-subfiles: parse basename from resolve/URI — never generic multi-file ambiguous.\"\"\"\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") if isinstance(resolve_data, dict) else None\n"
    "        resolved_name = \"\"\n"
    "        if isinstance(bt_info, dict):\n"
    "            resolved_name = str(bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        probe = (\n"
    "            resolved_name\n"
    "            or str(source.get(\"name\") or source.get(\"label\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            or _ed2k_uri_basename(\n"
    "                source.get(\"uri\")\n"
    "                or (resolve_data.get(\"url\") if isinstance(resolve_data, dict) else \"\")\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        diagnostics = [f\"ed2k_single_file:{probe or '-'}\"]\n"
    "        if not probe:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics,\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"ED2K 单文件无法取得文件名\",\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        if _is_subtitle(probe) and not _is_video(probe):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics + [\"subtitle_only\"],\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "                \"subtitle_only\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING: ED2K 单文件是字幕\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics,\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        missing = set(int(v) for v in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        reserved = set(int(v) for v in (self._pending_reservations(subscribe).get(\"episodes\") or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        configured_target = set()\n"
    "        for raw in source.get(\"target_episodes\") or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                configured_target.add(value)\n"
    "        # Prefer final_target from active EpisodeFacts context when present.\n"
    "        final_target: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            ctx_fn = getattr(self, \"_episode_run_context_for_subscribe_v211\", None)\n"
    "            ctx = ctx_fn(subscribe) if callable(ctx_fn) else None\n"
    "            if isinstance(ctx, dict):\n"
    "                final_target = {\n"
    "                    int(v) for v in (ctx.get(\"final_target\") or [])\n"
    "                    if int(v or 0) > 0\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            final_target = set()\n"
    "        base_target = final_target or missing\n"
    "        target = (configured_target or base_target).intersection(base_target) - reserved\n"
    "        if not target:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics + [\"covered_or_empty_target\"],\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "                \"covered\": True,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        season_hint = getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None)\n"
    "        episode_hint = str(source.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        threshold = float(self._episode_auto_confidence or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "        actual_episodes: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        result = resolve_episode(\n"
    "            probe,\n"
    "            package_paths=[probe],\n"
    "            season_hint=season_hint,\n"
    "            episode_hint=episode_hint,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        actual_episodes.update(reliable_episode_set(result, threshold))\n"
    "        if not actual_episodes and episode_hint:\n"
    "            hinted = resolve_episode(episode_hint, season_hint=season_hint)\n"
    "            actual_episodes.update(reliable_episode_set(hinted, 0.99))\n"
    "        matched = sorted(actual_episodes.intersection(target))\n"
    "        if matched:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": matched,\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics + [\n"
    "                    f\"{probe} => {','.join(f'E{v:02d}' for v in matched)}\"\n"
    "                ],\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        if actual_episodes and not actual_episodes.intersection(target):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                \"diagnostics\": diagnostics + [f\"out_of_target:{sorted(actual_episodes)}\"],\n"
    "                \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "                \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "                \"out_of_target\": True,\n"
    "                \"parsed_episodes\": sorted(actual_episodes),\n"
    "                \"message\": \"EPISODE_NOT_TARGET: ED2K 单文件不在当前 final_target\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "            \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "            \"diagnostics\": diagnostics,\n"
    "            \"ambiguous\": True,\n"
    "            \"ed2k_single_file\": True,\n"
    "            \"message\": f\"ED2K 单文件无法高置信映射当前缺集：{probe[:180]}\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _planner_file_selection(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        resolve_data: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        rows = self._media_rows(resolve_data)\n"
    "        media_rows = [row for row in rows if row[\"video\"] or row[\"subtitle\"]]\n"
    "        source_type = str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        bt_info = resolve_data.get(\"btResInfo\") if isinstance(resolve_data, dict) else None\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") if isinstance(bt_info, dict) else None\n"
    "        no_subfiles = not (isinstance(subfiles, list) and subfiles)\n"
    "        # ED2K without enumerable subfiles must NOT enter generic multi-file ambiguous path.\n"
    "        if source_type == \"ed2k\" and no_subfiles:\n"
    "            return self._ed2k_single_file_selection_v190(source, subscribe, resolve_data)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not rows:\n"
    "            return {\"indexes\": [], \"episodes\": [], \"diagnostics\": [], \"ambiguous\": False}\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 电影不存在“按集拆包”，继续尊重 media_only；有媒体文件时只保存媒体相关文件。\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            if bool(getattr(self, \"_media_only\", True)):\n"
    "                indexes = [int(row[\"index\"]) for row in media_rows]\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                indexes = [int(row[\"index\"]) for row in rows]\n"
    "            return {\"indexes\": list(dict.fromkeys(indexes)), \"episodes\": [], \"diagnostics\": [], \"ambiguous\": False}\n"
    "\n"
    "        missing = set(int(v) for v in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        reserved = set(int(v) for v in (self._pending_reservations(subscribe).get(\"episodes\") or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        configured_target = set()\n"
    "        for raw in source.get(\"target_episodes\") or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                configured_target.add(value)\n"
    "        target = (configured_target or missing).intersection(missing) - reserved\n"
    "        if not target:\n"
    "            return {\"indexes\": [], \"episodes\": [], \"diagnostics\": [\"当前目标剧集已被其它任务覆盖或已完成\"], \"ambiguous\": False, \"covered\": True}\n"
    "\n"
    "        package_paths = [str(row[\"name\"]) for row in media_rows]\n"
    "        season_hint = getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None)\n"
    "        episode_hint = str(source.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\")\n"
    "        threshold = float(self._episode_auto_confidence or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "\n"
    "        resolutions: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        diagnostics: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for row in media_rows:\n"
    "            result = resolve_episode(\n"
    "                row[\"name\"],\n"
    "                package_paths=package_paths,\n"
    "                season_hint=season_hint,\n"
    "                episode_hint=episode_hint,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            resolutions[int(row[\"index\"])] = result\n"
    "            eps = reliable_episode_set(result, threshold)\n"
    "            diagnostics.append(\n"
    "                f\"{row['name']} => {','.join('E%02d' % value for value in sorted(eps)) or '?'} \"\n"
    "                f\"({float(result.get('confidence') or 0):.2f}, {result.get('reason') or '-'})\"\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        selected_videos: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        covered: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        unresolved_videos: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for row in media_rows:\n"
    "            if not row[\"video\"]:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            result = resolutions.get(int(row[\"index\"])) or {}\n"
    "            episodes = reliable_episode_set(result, threshold)\n"
    "            hit = episodes.intersection(target)\n"
    "            if hit:\n"
    "                selected_videos.append(row)\n"
    "                covered.update(hit)\n"
    "            elif not episodes:\n"
    "                unresolved_videos.append(str(row[\"name\"]))\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 单文件 + 帖子明确单集时允许上下文推断；除此之外绝不把 A.mkv/B.mkv 按顺序猜成集号。\n"
    "        if not selected_videos:\n"
    "            video_rows = [row for row in media_rows if row[\"video\"]]\n"
    "            hint_result = resolve_episode(episode_hint, season_hint=season_hint) if episode_hint else {}\n"
    "            hinted = reliable_episode_set(hint_result, 0.99)\n"
    "            if len(video_rows) == 1 and len(target) == 1 and hinted == target:\n"
    "                selected_videos = [video_rows[0]]\n"
    "                covered = set(target)\n"
    "                diagnostics.append(f\"{video_rows[0]['name']} => 上下文明示单集 {sorted(target)}\")\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                detail = \"；\".join(unresolved_videos[:6]) or \"没有文件能高置信映射到当前缺集\"\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"indexes\": [],\n"
    "                    \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                    \"diagnostics\": diagnostics,\n"
    "                    \"ambiguous\": True,\n"
    "                    \"message\": detail,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "\n"
    "        indexes: List[int] = [int(row[\"index\"]) for row in selected_videos]\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 字幕必须能映射到已选择剧集；弱命名字幕仅在同目录只有一个已选视频时保守跟随。\n"
    "        videos_by_parent: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n"
    "        for row in selected_videos:\n"
    "            videos_by_parent.setdefault(self._parent_path(str(row[\"name\"])), []).append(row)\n"
    "        for row in media_rows:\n"
    "            if not row[\"subtitle\"]:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            result = resolutions.get(int(row[\"index\"])) or {}\n"
    "            episodes = reliable_episode_set(result, threshold)\n"
    "            if episodes.intersection(covered):\n"
    "                indexes.append(int(row[\"index\"]))\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            parent = self._parent_path(str(row[\"name\"]))\n"
    "            if not episodes and len(videos_by_parent.get(parent) or []) == 1:\n"
    "                indexes.append(int(row[\"index\"]))\n"
    "\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"indexes\": list(dict.fromkeys(indexes)),\n"
    "            \"episodes\": sorted(covered),\n"
    "            \"diagnostics\": diagnostics,\n"
    "            \"ambiguous\": False,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        response = self._offline_request(\n"
    "            \"/cloudcollection/v1/resolve_res\",\n"
    "            {\"url\": str(source.get(\"uri\") or \"\")},\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not self._offline_api_success(response):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(self._offline_api_error(response, \"光鸭云添加解析失败\"))\n"
    "        data = self._offline_resolved_data(response)\n"
    "        bt_info = data.get(\"btResInfo\") or {}\n"
    "        resolved_name = \"\"\n"
    "        if isinstance(bt_info, dict):\n"
    "            resolved_name = str(bt_info.get(\"fileName\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        resolved_name = resolved_name or str(source.get(\"name\") or source.get(\"label\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        resolved_url = str(data.get(\"url\") or source.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "        selection = self._planner_file_selection(source, subscribe, data)\n"
    "        indexes = list(selection.get(\"indexes\") or [])\n"
    "        subfiles = bt_info.get(\"subfiles\") if isinstance(bt_info, dict) else None\n"
    "\n"
    "        # ED2K 通常是“单文件云添加”，resolve_res 不一定返回 btResInfo.subfiles。\n"
    "        # 旧逻辑在这种情况下会创建任务，但 resolved_episodes 为空；完成后无法把该集\n"
    "        # 写回 MoviePilot，也可能让后续扫描再次命中同一集。现在用真实 resolve 文件名\n"
    "        # + 频道 episode_hint 做最后一次高置信集号确认，并把实际命中的缺集回填。\n"
    "        source_type = str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "        no_subfiles = not (isinstance(subfiles, list) and subfiles)\n"
    "        # ED2K no-subfiles: require resolved filename to be a real video before submit.\n"
    "        if source_type == \"ed2k\" and no_subfiles:\n"
    "            if bool(selection.get(\"subtitle_only\")):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING: \"\n"
    "                    f\"ED2K 单文件是字幕：{str(selection.get('message') or resolved_name or '-')[:180]}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            if bool(selection.get(\"out_of_target\")):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}EPISODE_NOT_TARGET: \"\n"
    "                    f\"ED2K 单文件不在当前目标：{sorted(selection.get('parsed_episodes') or [])}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            probe_name = (\n"
    "                resolved_name\n"
    "                or str(source.get(\"name\") or source.get(\"label\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                or _ed2k_uri_basename(source.get(\"uri\") or resolved_url)\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if not _is_video(probe_name):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                    f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}SELECTED_VIDEO_MISSING: \"\n"
    "                    f\"ED2K 单文件不是正片视频：{str(probe_name or '-')[:180]}\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "\n"
    "        if source_type == \"ed2k\" and not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) and no_subfiles:\n"
    "            # Prefer selection already built by single-file planner (ambiguous cleared there).\n"
    "            matched_from_planner = {\n"
    "                int(value) for value in (selection.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "                if int(value or 0) > 0\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            if matched_from_planner and not bool(selection.get(\"ambiguous\")):\n"
    "                selection[\"episodes\"] = sorted(matched_from_planner)\n"
    "                selection[\"ambiguous\"] = False\n"
    "                selection.pop(\"message\", None)\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道云添加】ED2K 单文件解析命中缺集=%s，允许提交光鸭 cloudcollection\",\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in sorted(matched_from_planner)),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                season_hint = getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None)\n"
    "                episode_hint = str(source.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                actual_names = []\n"
    "                for value in (\n"
    "                    resolved_name,\n"
    "                    bt_info.get(\"fileName\") if isinstance(bt_info, dict) else \"\",\n"
    "                    _ed2k_uri_basename(source.get(\"uri\") or resolved_url),\n"
    "                ):\n"
    "                    value = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                    if value and value not in actual_names:\n"
    "                        actual_names.append(value)\n"
    "                actual_episodes = set()\n"
    "                threshold = float(self._episode_auto_confidence or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE)\n"
    "                for value in actual_names:\n"
    "                    result = resolve_episode(\n"
    "                        value,\n"
    "                        package_paths=actual_names,\n"
    "                        season_hint=season_hint,\n"
    "                        episode_hint=episode_hint,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    actual_episodes.update(reliable_episode_set(result, threshold))\n"
    "                if not actual_episodes and episode_hint:\n"
    "                    hinted = resolve_episode(episode_hint, season_hint=season_hint)\n"
    "                    actual_episodes.update(reliable_episode_set(hinted, 0.99))\n"
    "\n"
    "                missing_now = {\n"
    "                    int(value) for value in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "                    if int(value or 0) > 0\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                configured_target = {\n"
    "                    int(value) for value in (source.get(\"target_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "                    if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                allowed_target = (configured_target or missing_now).intersection(missing_now)\n"
    "                matched_episodes = actual_episodes.intersection(allowed_target)\n"
    "                if not matched_episodes:\n"
    "                    detail = \", \".join(actual_names[:2]) or str(source.get(\"name\") or \"ED2K 单文件\")\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(\n"
    "                        f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}ED2K 已解析但真实文件无法确认覆盖当前缺集：{detail}\"\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                # CRITICAL: clear any stale ambiguous from earlier generic planner paths.\n"
    "                selection[\"episodes\"] = sorted(matched_episodes)\n"
    "                selection[\"ambiguous\"] = False\n"
    "                selection.pop(\"message\", None)\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道云添加】ED2K 单文件解析命中缺集=%s，允许提交光鸭 cloudcollection\",\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in sorted(matched_episodes)),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        if not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) and bool(selection.get(\"covered\")) and not indexes:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}当前缺集已被其它在途任务覆盖，暂不创建重复离线任务\")\n"
    "        if bool(selection.get(\"ambiguous\")):\n"
    "            detail = str(selection.get(\"message\") or \"文件集号无法可靠识别\")\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}{detail}\")\n"
    "        if bool(getattr(self, \"_media_only\", True)) and isinstance(subfiles, list) and subfiles and not indexes:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"{_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX}光鸭已解析来源，但没有高置信匹配当前缺集的视频文件\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        manifest = self._assert_selected_video_present_v210(\n"
    "            resolve_data=data,\n"
    "            selected_indexes=indexes,\n"
    "            source=source,\n"
    "            subscribe=subscribe,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if source_id:\n"
    "            self._update_source(\n"
    "                source_id,\n"
    "                resolved_episodes=list(selection.get(\"episodes\") or []),\n"
    "                selection_diagnostics=list(selection.get(\"diagnostics\") or [])[:80],\n"
    "                selection_confidence=float(self._episode_auto_confidence or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE),\n"
    "                selected_manifest=manifest[:80],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"resolved_name\": resolved_name[:300],\n"
    "            \"resolved_url\": resolved_url,\n"
    "            \"selected_indexes\": indexes,\n"
    "            \"selected_manifest\": manifest,\n"
    "            \"resolve_data\": data,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _mark_offline_failure(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        error: Exception | str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        attempt_increment: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        message = str(error or \"\")\n"
    "        if message.startswith(_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX):\n"
    "            detail = message[len(_AMBIGUOUS_PREFIX):].strip() or \"集号无法可靠识别\"\n"
    "            updated = self._update_source(\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                state=\"needs_review\",\n"
    "                last_error=detail[:500],\n"
    "                next_retry_at=0,\n"
    "            ) or source\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【拆包保护】来源 %s 未创建离线任务：%s\",\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                detail,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return updated\n"
    "        return super()._mark_offline_failure(source, error, attempt_increment=attempt_increment)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _poll_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = super()._poll_offline_source(source)\n"
    "        data = result.get(\"data\") if isinstance(result, dict) else None\n"
    "        if not isinstance(data, dict) or str(data.get(\"state\") or \"\") != \"completed\":\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        latest = dict(self._source_store()[\"items\"].get(str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")) or data)\n"
    "        episodes = []\n"
    "        for raw in latest.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                episodes.append(value)\n"
    "        if episodes:\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(int(latest.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0))\n"
    "            if subscribe:\n"
    "                self._remember_episode_facts(subscribe, episodes, origin=\"guangya_offline\")\n"
    "                self._sync_media_facts_progress(subscribe)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 同一资源多候选的决策与回退\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _candidate_target_episodes(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        entry: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        uncovered: set[int],\n"
    "    ) -> set[int]:\n"
    "        source_name = str(source.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "        hint = str(entry.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\")\n"
    "        result = resolve_episode(\n"
    "            source_name,\n"
    "            season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None),\n"
    "            episode_hint=hint,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        explicit = reliable_episode_set(result, float(self._episode_auto_confidence or AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE))\n"
    "        if explicit:\n"
    "            return explicit.intersection(uncovered)\n"
    "\n"
    "        hint_result = resolve_episode(hint, season_hint=getattr(subscribe, \"season\", None)) if hint else {}\n"
    "        hinted = reliable_episode_set(hint_result, 0.99)\n"
    "        if hinted:\n"
    "            return hinted.intersection(uncovered)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # Magnet/ED2K 都允许先进入光鸭 resolve：\n"
    "        # - Magnet 常见整季/更新包，需要解析内部文件后才能确认缺集；\n"
    "        # - ED2K 是单文件链接，频道标题或文件名可能不带可直接识别的集号，\n"
    "        #   但 resolve 后的真实文件名仍可安全确认。这里只做“待解析候选”，\n"
    "        #   最终集号仍必须在 _resolve_offline_source 中回填并通过缺集门禁。\n"
    "        if str(source.get(\"type\") or \"\") in {\"magnet\", \"ed2k\"}:\n"
    "            return set(uncovered)\n"
    "        return set()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _save_resource_plan(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        sid = str(int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0))\n"
    "        plans = self.get_data(\"resource_plans\") or {}\n"
    "        plans[sid] = {\n"
    "            **dict(payload or {}),\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": int(sid or 0),\n"
    "            \"name\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if len(plans) > 500:\n"
    "            ordered = sorted(plans.items(), key=lambda pair: str((pair[1] or {}).get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"), reverse=True)[:500]\n"
    "            plans = dict(ordered)\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"resource_plans\", plans)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_channel_external_candidates(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if not sid or not self._channel_external_auto_dispatch:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"actions\": [], \"message\": \"频道外部候选自动调度已关闭\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        is_movie = self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe)\n"
    "        reservations = self._pending_reservations(subscribe)\n"
    "        if is_movie and bool(reservations.get(\"movie\")):\n"
    "            self._save_resource_plan(subscribe, {\"movie\": True, \"waiting_share\": True, \"actions\": []})\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"actions\": [], \"message\": \"光鸭直接转存正在落盘，外部候选保持备用\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        missing = set(int(v) for v in (self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        reserved = set(int(v) for v in (reservations.get(\"episodes\") or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0)\n"
    "        active_claims = self._active_source_claims(sid)\n"
    "        uncovered = missing - reserved - active_claims\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not is_movie and not uncovered:\n"
    "            self._save_resource_plan(subscribe, {\n"
    "                \"missing\": sorted(missing),\n"
    "                \"reserved_by_share\": sorted(reserved),\n"
    "                \"claimed_by_external\": sorted(active_claims),\n"
    "                \"uncovered\": [],\n"
    "                \"actions\": [],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"actions\": [], \"message\": \"当前缺集已由已完成/在途任务覆盖\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 电影已有任何活跃外部来源时不再创建第二个候选任务。\n"
    "        if is_movie:\n"
    "            active_movie = any(\n"
    "                isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "                and int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) == sid\n"
    "                and str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\") in _ACTIVE_CLAIM_STATES\n"
    "                for row in self._source_store()[\"items\"].values()\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if active_movie:\n"
    "                return {\"success\": True, \"actions\": [], \"message\": \"电影已有外部获取任务\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "        entries = list((self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}).get(\"items\") or [])\n"
    "        matched_entries = []\n"
    "        for entry in entries:\n"
    "            external = list(entry.get(\"external_sources\") or []) if isinstance(entry, dict) else []\n"
    "            if not external or entry.get(\"stale\"):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            matched, reason = _entry_match_reason(entry, subscribe)\n"
    "            if matched:\n"
    "                matched_entries.append((entry, reason))\n"
    "\n"
    "        actions = []\n"
    "        skipped = []\n"
    "        magnet_selected = False\n"
    "        dispatch_busy = False\n"
    "        for entry, match_reason in matched_entries:\n"
    "            external = list(entry.get(\"external_sources\") or [])\n"
    "            external.sort(key=lambda row: 0 if str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\") == \"magnet\" else 1)\n"
    "            for candidate in external:\n"
    "                source_type = str(candidate.get(\"type\") or \"\")\n"
    "                if source_type not in {\"magnet\", \"ed2k\"}:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if magnet_selected and source_type == \"magnet\":\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                allowed, rule_reason = self._external_resource_allowed(subscribe, entry, candidate)\n"
    "                if not allowed:\n"
    "                    skipped.append({\"type\": source_type, \"reason\": rule_reason})\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "\n"
    "                existing = self._existing_source(sid, source_type, str(candidate.get(\"identity\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                existing_state = str(existing.get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "                if existing_state in {\"failed\", \"needs_review\", \"disabled\"}:\n"
    "                    # 已确认不可用的候选不阻断本组下一种方式，实现 Magnet -> ED2K 回退。\n"
    "                    skipped.append({\"type\": source_type, \"reason\": f\"已有候选状态 {existing_state}，尝试下一候选\"})\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if existing and existing_state in _ACTIVE_CLAIM_STATES:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "\n"
    "                if is_movie:\n"
    "                    target = set()\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    target = self._candidate_target_episodes(subscribe, entry, candidate, uncovered)\n"
    "                    if not target:\n"
    "                        continue\n"
    "\n"
    "                rank = 1 if source_type == \"magnet\" else 2\n"
    "                uri = str(candidate.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                identity = str(candidate.get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                resource_trace = str(entry.get(\"resource_trace_id\") or entry.get(\"trace_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    from .transfer_diag_v209 import make_candidate_trace_id\n"
    "                    if source_type == \"magnet\":\n"
    "                        cand_trace = make_candidate_trace_id(\"magnet\", identity or uri)\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        # ed2k: prefer hash:size when present in identity/uri\n"
    "                        cand_trace = make_candidate_trace_id(\"ed2k\", identity or uri)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    cand_trace = f\"{source_type}:{identity or uri[:40]}\"\n"
    "                # Run-level episode claim before cloudcollection task creation.\n"
    "                claim_fn = getattr(self, \"_claim_run_episodes_v209\", None)\n"
    "                release_fn = getattr(self, \"_release_run_episodes_v209\", None)\n"
    "                mark_fn = getattr(self, \"_mark_run_episodes_state_v209\", None)\n"
    "                note_fn = getattr(self, \"_note_candidate_diag_v209\", None) or getattr(self, \"_append_candidate_diag_v209\", None)\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    season = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    season = 0\n"
    "                if callable(claim_fn):\n"
    "                    if is_movie:\n"
    "                        claimed, blocked = claim_fn(\n"
    "                            season=0,\n"
    "                            episodes=[0],\n"
    "                            candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                            sid=sid,\n"
    "                            movie=True,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                        if blocked and not claimed:\n"
    "                            if callable(note_fn):\n"
    "                                try:\n"
    "                                    from .transfer_diag_v209 import make_diag\n"
    "                                    note_fn(make_diag(\n"
    "                                        state=\"PENDING\",\n"
    "                                        reason_code=\"TRANSFER_ALREADY_RESERVED\",\n"
    "                                        stage=\"SOURCE_SELECTION\",\n"
    "                                        source=source_type,\n"
    "                                        message=\"同轮电影主任务已被其它候选占用\",\n"
    "                                        resource_trace_id=resource_trace,\n"
    "                                        candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                                        sid=sid,\n"
    "                                    ))\n"
    "                                except Exception:\n"
    "                                    pass\n"
    "                            skipped.append({\"type\": source_type, \"reason\": \"TRANSFER_ALREADY_RESERVED\"})\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        claimed, blocked = claim_fn(\n"
    "                            season=season,\n"
    "                            episodes=sorted(int(v) for v in target),\n"
    "                            candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                            sid=sid,\n"
    "                            movie=False,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                        if blocked:\n"
    "                            if callable(note_fn):\n"
    "                                try:\n"
    "                                    from .transfer_diag_v209 import make_diag\n"
    "                                    note_fn(make_diag(\n"
    "                                        state=\"PENDING\" if claimed else \"SKIPPED\",\n"
    "                                        reason_code=\"TRANSFER_ALREADY_RESERVED\",\n"
    "                                        stage=\"SOURCE_SELECTION\",\n"
    "                                        source=source_type,\n"
    "                                        message=f\"同轮 episode 已被其它候选占用：{sorted(set(blocked))}\",\n"
    "                                        resource_trace_id=resource_trace,\n"
    "                                        candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                                        evidence={\"blocked_episodes\": sorted(set(blocked)), \"claimed_episodes\": sorted(set(claimed))},\n"
    "                                        sid=sid,\n"
    "                                    ))\n"
    "                                except Exception:\n"
    "                                    pass\n"
    "                        if not claimed:\n"
    "                            skipped.append({\"type\": source_type, \"reason\": \"TRANSFER_ALREADY_RESERVED\"})\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                        target = set(claimed)\n"
    "                run_id = \"\"\n"
    "                getter = getattr(self, \"_current_subscription_run_id\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(getter):\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        run_id = str(getter() or \"\")\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        run_id = \"\"\n"
    "                row = self._upsert_source(\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    uri,\n"
    "                    label=str(candidate.get(\"name\") or \"\")[:120],\n"
    "                    origin=\"telegram\",\n"
    "                    auto_dispatch=True,\n"
    "                    resource_group_id=str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    target_episodes=sorted(target),\n"
    "                    episode_hint=str(entry.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    source_label=str(entry.get(\"source_label\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    message_id=str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    candidate_rank=rank,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                # Persist cross-thread diagnostic context on the source row.\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    self._update_source(\n"
    "                        str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                        resource_trace_id=resource_trace,\n"
    "                        candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                        subscription_run_id=run_id,\n"
    "                        diag_identity=identity,\n"
    "                        diag_uri=uri,\n"
    "                        passcode_present=bool(candidate.get(\"passcode\")),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "                action = {\n"
    "                    \"resource_group_id\": str(entry.get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"source_id\": str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"type\": source_type,\n"
    "                    \"uri\": uri,\n"
    "                    \"identity\": identity,\n"
    "                    \"resource_trace_id\": resource_trace,\n"
    "                    \"candidate_trace_id\": cand_trace,\n"
    "                    \"subscription_run_id\": run_id,\n"
    "                    \"message_id\": str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"episodes\": sorted(target),\n"
    "                    \"reason\": f\"{match_reason}；光鸭直接转存未覆盖这些目标集；候选优先级 {rank}\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                dispatch = self._spawn_source_dispatch(str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\")) or {}\n"
    "                if not dispatch.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                    if callable(release_fn) and target:\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            release_fn(\n"
    "                                season=0 if is_movie else season,\n"
    "                                episodes=[0] if is_movie else sorted(target),\n"
    "                                candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                                movie=is_movie,\n"
    "                            )\n"
    "                        except Exception:\n"
    "                            pass\n"
    "                    skipped.append({\"type\": source_type, \"reason\": str(dispatch.get(\"message\") or \"来源未入队\")})\n"
    "                    if dispatch.get(\"reason\") == \"already_running\":\n"
    "                        dispatch_busy = True\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if callable(mark_fn) and target:\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        mark_fn(\n"
    "                            season=0 if is_movie else season,\n"
    "                            episodes=[0] if is_movie else sorted(target),\n"
    "                            candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                            state=\"pending\",\n"
    "                            movie=is_movie,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "                actions.append(action)\n"
    "                if source_type == \"magnet\":\n"
    "                    magnet_selected = True\n"
    "                if not is_movie:\n"
    "                    uncovered -= target\n"
    "                if is_movie or not uncovered:\n"
    "                    break\n"
    "            if dispatch_busy or (is_movie and actions) or (not is_movie and not uncovered):\n"
    "                break\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._save_resource_plan(subscribe, {\n"
    "            \"missing\": sorted(missing),\n"
    "            \"reserved_by_share\": sorted(reserved),\n"
    "            \"claimed_by_external\": sorted(active_claims),\n"
    "            \"uncovered\": sorted(uncovered),\n"
    "            \"actions\": actions,\n"
    "            \"skipped\": skipped[:30],\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        if actions:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"actions\": actions, \"message\": f\"已生成 {len(actions)} 个外部候选执行计划\"}\n"
    "        return {\"success\": False, \"actions\": [], \"message\": \"当前没有可安全执行的 Magnet/ED2K 候选\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        force: bool = False,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        share_result = super()._try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            force=force,\n"
    "            refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            external = self._dispatch_channel_external_candidates(subscribe)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【资源决策】#%s 外部候选规划失败：%s\", getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0), err)\n"
    "            external = {\"success\": False, \"actions\": [], \"message\": str(err)}\n"
    "        share_result = dict(share_result or {})\n"
    "        candidate_diags = list(share_result.get(\"candidate_diags\") or [])\n"
    "        share_success = bool(share_result.get(\"success\")) and not bool(share_result.get(\"pending\")) and not bool(share_result.get(\"partial\"))\n"
    "        actions = list(external.get(\"actions\") or [])\n"
    "        if actions:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                from .transfer_diag_v209 import make_candidate_trace_id, make_diag\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                make_diag = None\n"
    "                make_candidate_trace_id = None\n"
    "            for action in actions:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(action, dict) or make_diag is None:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                src = str(action.get(\"type\") or action.get(\"source\") or action.get(\"protocol\") or \"magnet\").lower()\n"
    "                identity = str(action.get(\"identity\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                uri = str(action.get(\"uri\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if not identity and uri:\n"
    "                    identity = uri\n"
    "                resource_trace = str(action.get(\"resource_trace_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                cand_trace = str(action.get(\"candidate_trace_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "                if not cand_trace and make_candidate_trace_id is not None:\n"
    "                    cand_trace = make_candidate_trace_id(src, identity or uri)\n"
    "                if share_success:\n"
    "                    note = make_diag(\n"
    "                        state=\"SKIPPED\",\n"
    "                        reason_code=\"SUPERSEDED_BY_HIGHER_PRIORITY_SOURCE\",\n"
    "                        stage=\"SOURCE_SELECTION\",\n"
    "                        source=src,\n"
    "                        message=\"更高优先级来源已成功，本候选不再执行\",\n"
    "                        candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                        resource_trace_id=resource_trace,\n"
    "                        subscription_run_id=str(action.get(\"subscription_run_id\") or share_result.get(\"subscription_run_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                        sid=getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                        evidence={\"uri\": uri[:160], \"identity\": identity[:120], \"message_id\": action.get(\"message_id\")},\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    note = make_diag(\n"
    "                        state=\"PENDING\",\n"
    "                        reason_code=\"REMOTE_TASK_PENDING\",\n"
    "                        stage=\"SUBMIT\",\n"
    "                        source=src,\n"
    "                        message=str(action.get(\"reason\") or \"外部候选已入队待远端确认\"),\n"
    "                        candidate_trace_id=cand_trace,\n"
    "                        resource_trace_id=resource_trace,\n"
    "                        subscription_run_id=str(action.get(\"subscription_run_id\") or share_result.get(\"subscription_run_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                        sid=getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0),\n"
    "                        evidence={\"uri\": uri[:160], \"identity\": identity[:120], \"message_id\": action.get(\"message_id\")},\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                note_fn = getattr(self, \"_note_candidate_diag_v209\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(note_fn):\n"
    "                    note_fn(note, candidate_diags)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    candidate_diags.append(note)\n"
    "            share_result[\"candidate_diags\"] = candidate_diags\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                **share_result,\n"
    "                \"success\": True if actions and not share_success else bool(share_result.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"pending\": True if actions and not share_success else bool(share_result.get(\"pending\")),\n"
    "                \"external_actions\": actions,\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"{str(share_result.get('message') or '光鸭分享已检查')}；{external.get('message')}\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        if candidate_diags:\n"
    "            share_result[\"candidate_diags\"] = candidate_diags\n"
    "        return share_result\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # API / 状态页\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def api_resource_plan(self, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        plans = self.get_data(\"resource_plans\") or {}\n"
    "        sid = str(int(subscribe_id or 0))\n"
    "        if sid != \"0\":\n"
    "            row = dict(plans.get(sid) or {})\n"
    "            return {\"success\": bool(row), \"data\": row}\n"
    "        rows = [dict(row) for row in plans.values() if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda row: str(row.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"), reverse=True)\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"count\": len(rows), \"data\": rows[:100]}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_api(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        apis = list(super().get_api() or [])\n"
    "        if not any(str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\") == \"/resource/plan\" for item in apis if isinstance(item, dict)):\n"
    "            apis.append({\n"
    "                \"path\": \"/resource/plan\",\n"
    "                \"endpoint\": self.api_resource_plan,\n"
    "                \"methods\": [\"GET\"],\n"
    "                \"summary\": \"查看 ResourceGroup 缺集决策计划\",\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return apis\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_form(self):\n"
    "        form, defaults = super().get_form()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            content = form[0].get(\"content\") if form else None\n"
    "            if isinstance(content, list):\n"
    "                content.append({\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mt-3\"},\n"
    "                    \"content\": [\n"
    "                        {\"component\": \"VCardTitle\", \"text\": \"资源决策与安全拆包\"},\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                            \"text\": (\n"
    "                                \"同一频道消息按 ResourceGroup 处理：光鸭直接转存优先，Magnet 次之，ED2K 兜底。\"\n"
    "                                \"剧集只在集号置信度达到阈值时自动拆包；无法可靠识别时不会整包误存。\"\n"
    "                            ),\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VRow\",\n"
    "                            \"content\": [\n"
    "                                {\n"
    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 12, \"md\": 6},\n"
    "                                    \"content\": [{\n"
    "                                        \"component\": \"VSwitch\",\n"
    "                                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                                            \"model\": \"channel_external_auto_dispatch\",\n"
    "                                            \"label\": \"频道 Magnet/ED2K 自动候选\",\n"
    "                                        },\n"
    "                                    }],\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                                {\n"
    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 12, \"md\": 6},\n"
    "                                    \"content\": [{\n"
    "                                        \"component\": \"VTextField\",\n"
    "                                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                                            \"model\": \"episode_auto_confidence\",\n"
    "                                            \"label\": \"自动拆包置信度\",\n"
    "                                            \"type\": \"number\",\n"
    "                                            \"step\": \"0.01\",\n"
    "                                            \"min\": \"0.80\",\n"
    "                                            \"max\": \"1.00\",\n"
    "                                        },\n"
    "                                    }],\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                            ],\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        defaults.update({\n"
    "            \"channel_external_auto_dispatch\": self._channel_external_auto_dispatch,\n"
    "            \"episode_auto_confidence\": self._episode_auto_confidence,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        pages = list(super().get_page() or [])\n"
    "        plans = self.api_resource_plan().get(\"data\") or []\n"
    "        unresolved = [row for row in plans if row.get(\"uncovered\")]\n"
    "        recent_actions = sum(len(row.get(\"actions\") or []) for row in plans[:20])\n"
    "        card = {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\n"
    "                \"type\": \"warning\" if unresolved else \"success\",\n"
    "                \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                \"title\": \"资源组决策 · 缺集拆包\",\n"
    "                \"text\": (\n"
    "                    f\"最近计划 {len(plans)} 个 · 已生成外部执行 {recent_actions} 个 · \"\n"
    "                    f\"仍有未覆盖 {len(unresolved)} 个订阅。\"\n"
    "                    \" 决策顺序：光鸭直接转存 > Magnet > ED2K；低置信集号不自动保存。\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        return [card, *pages]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaResourcePlannerMixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== routing_v170.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["routing_v170"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.7：全入口硬分流、消息直订与无刷新路由切换。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.6.x 的成熟频道解析、分享检查、增量转存和进度闭环保留在 legacy.py。\n"
    "本文件只叠加路由边界与交互体验，避免再次复制转存业务实现。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"

    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple\n"

    "from app.chain.subscribe import SubscribeChain\n"
    "from app.db.oper.subscribe import SubscribeOper\n"
