    "\n"
    "    def _canonical_transfer_name_v11226(\n"
    "        self,\n"

    "        original: Any,\n"
    "        *,\n"

    "\n"
    "    def _resolve_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any], subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        resolved = dict(super()._resolve_offline_source(source, subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        original = str(\n"
    "            source.get(\"original_resolved_name\")\n"
    "            or resolved.get(\"resolved_name\")\n"
    "            or source.get(\"resolved_name\")\n"

    "    def _rapid_transfer_xunlei_file(self, subscribe: Any, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        prepared = dict(row or {})\n"
    "        old_name = str(prepared.get(\"name\") or str(prepared.get(\"path\") or \"\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1] or \"file\").strip()\n"
    "        path_hint = str(prepared.get(\"path\") or old_name)\n"
    "        desired = self._canonical_transfer_name_v11226(subscribe, path_hint)\n"

    "            prepared[\"name\"] = desired\n"

    "            parent = raw_path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0] if \"/\" in raw_path else \"\"\n"
    "            prepared[\"path\"] = f\"{parent}/{desired}\" if parent else desired\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【命名v1.12.26】迅雷秒传名称：%s -> %s\",\n"

    "                desired[:220],\n"
    "            )\n"

    "        from .auto_recovery_v11224 import GuangYaAutoRecoveryV11224Mixin\n"

    "        parent = super(GuangYaAutoRecoveryV11224Mixin, self)\n"

    "\n"
    "    def _rename_restored_media_v11224(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        save_path: str,\n"
    "        items: Iterable[Dict[str, Any]],\n"
    "    ) -> int:\n"
    "        \"\"\"覆盖 v1.12.24 前缀命名：落盘后改为剧集名 + 季集。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if subscribe is None:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        client, api = self._get_guangya_runtime()\n"
    "        if not client or not api or not callable(getattr(client, \"rename\", None)):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        groups: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}\n"
    "        for item in items or []:\n"
    "            path = str(item.get(\"effective_path\") or item.get(\"relative_path\") or item.get(\"name\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if not (_is_video(path) or _is_subtitle(path)):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            groups.setdefault(str(item.get(\"target_parent\") or \"\"), []).append(dict(item))\n"
    "        renamed = 0\n"
    "        base = str(save_path or \"/\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n"
    "        rename_ok = getattr(self, \"_rename_ok_v11224\", None)\n"
    "        for relative_parent, group in groups.items():\n"
    "            parent = _safe_relative_path(relative_parent)\n"
    "            folder_path = (base.rstrip(\"/\") + (\"/\" + parent if parent else \"\")) or \"/\"\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                folder = api.get_item(Path(folder_path)) if callable(getattr(api, \"get_item\", None)) else None\n"
    "                if folder is None:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                remote_rows = list(api.list(folder) or []) if callable(getattr(api, \"list\", None)) else []\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【命名v1.12.26】读取已转存目录失败：%s (%s)\",\n"
    "                    folder_path,\n"
    "                    str(err)[:180],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            remote_by_name = {str(getattr(row, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"): row for row in remote_rows}\n"
    "            used_names = set(remote_by_name.keys())\n"
    "            for item in group:\n"
    "                path = str(item.get(\"effective_path\") or item.get(\"relative_path\") or item.get(\"name\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n"
    "                old_name = path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "                desired = self._canonical_transfer_name_v11226(subscribe, path)\n"
    "                if not desired or desired == old_name:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if desired in used_names and desired != old_name:\n"
    "                    stem, ext = _split_name_ext_v11226(desired)\n"
    "                    index = 2\n"
    "                    candidate = desired\n"
    "                    while candidate in used_names:\n"
    "                        candidate = f\"{stem} ({index}){ext}\"\n"
    "                        index += 1\n"
    "                    desired = candidate\n"
    "                remote = remote_by_name.get(old_name)\n"
    "                file_id = str(getattr(remote, \"fileid\", \"\") or \"\") if remote is not None else \"\"\n"
    "                if not file_id:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    response = client.rename(file_id, desired)\n"
    "                    ok = bool(rename_ok(response)) if callable(rename_ok) else True\n"
    "                    if ok:\n"
    "                        renamed += 1\n"
    "                        used_names.discard(old_name)\n"
    "                        used_names.add(desired)\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【命名v1.12.26】分享落盘后重命名：%s -> %s\",\n"
    "                            old_name[:180],\n"
    "                            desired[:220],\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【命名v1.12.26】分享已成功但重命名未确认：%s -> %s\",\n"
    "                            old_name[:160],\n"
    "                            desired[:200],\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                except Exception as err:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【命名v1.12.26】分享已成功但重命名异常：%s\",\n"
    "                        str(err)[:220],\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "        return renamed\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaChannelTitleRenameV11226Mixin\",\n"
    "    \"install_channel_title_rename_v11226\",\n"
    "    \"_canonical_transfer_name_v11226\",\n"
    "    \"_release_tail_v11226\",\n"

    "]\n"

# ===== xunlei_season_fence_v11210.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["xunlei_season_fence_v11210"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.10 迅雷季包物理资源栅栏。\n"
    "\n"
    "修复同一真实迅雷分享被同一剧集的多个 MoviePilot season 订阅同时消费，导致光鸭端出现\n"
    "01.mp4 / 01(1).mp4 这类物理重复文件的问题。\n"
    "\n"
    "安全原则：\n"
    "1. TV 无明确季号的包若解析到超过订阅本季总集数的集号，整包拒绝，禁止先裁剪目标集再\n"
    "   把其它季资源冒充当前季；\n"
    "2. 无明确季号的同一迅雷 share，在同一系列内首个真实成功 season 会取得持久 claim；\n"
    "   之后其它订阅/season 不得再次消费同一物理 share；同一订阅重试保持幂等；\n"
    "3. 明确带 Sxx/Season/第x季 的真实多季包不使用这个 seasonless claim，由既有文件级 planner\n"
    "   按显式季号拆分；\n"
    "4. 同一系列的迅雷入口串行化，确保两个 season 不会同时越过 claim 门禁；\n"
    "5. 只在光鸭确认某个迅雷文件真实成功后写 claim，失败候选不会永久占坑。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层不修改 GYING 搜索、迅雷 JSON 协议、质量过滤、MoviePilot 订阅规则或来源优先级。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"

    "import threading\n"

    "from .legacy import _is_video\n"
    "from .media_identity_v1111 import explicit_seasons_v1111\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaXunleiSeasonFenceV11210Mixin:\n"

    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # identity / package helpers\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _safe_positive_int_v11210(value: Any) -> int:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = int(value or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        return parsed if parsed > 0 else 0\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_series_key_v11210(self, subscribe: Any) -> str:\n"

    "            return None\n"
    "        with self._xunlei_series_lock_guard_v11210:\n"

    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_write_success_claim_v11210(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        share_id: str,\n"
    "        path: str,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"仅真实成功文件触发；首个 owner 永不被后来的订阅覆盖。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not subscribe or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        share_id = str(share_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        path = str(path or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "        if not share_id or not path or explicit_seasons_v1111([path]):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        series = self._xunlei_series_key_v11210(subscribe)\n"
    "        season = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0))\n"
    "        sid = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0))\n"
    "        if not series or season <= 0 or sid <= 0:\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        key = f\"{series}|{share_id}\"\n"
    "        with self._xunlei_claim_guard_v11210:\n"
    "            store = self._xunlei_claim_store_v11210()\n"
    "            items = store[\"items\"]\n"
    "            existing = dict(items.get(key) or {})\n"
    "            if existing:\n"
    "                return existing\n"
    "            row = {\n"
    "                \"series\": series,\n"
    "                \"share_id\": share_id,\n"
    "                \"season\": season,\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"path\": path[:500],\n"
    "                \"claimed_at\": time.time(),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            items[key] = row\n"
    "            self.save_data(self._xunlei_season_claim_data_v11210, store)\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷季栅栏v1.12.10】#%s S%02d 已取得无季号 share=%s 物理资源 claim\",\n"
    "            sid,\n"
    "            season,\n"
    "            share_id[:24],\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_recover_legacy_claim_v11210(self, subscribe: Any, share_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"兼容升级前成功状态：从 xunlei_flash_state 回推同系列 share 的首个成功 owner。\"\"\"\n"
    "        series = self._xunlei_series_key_v11210(subscribe)\n"
    "        share_id = str(share_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not series or not share_id:\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            state = self._xunlei_state()\n"
    "            items = list((state.get(\"items\") or {}).values()) if isinstance(state, dict) else []\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        rows = sorted(\n"
    "            (row for row in items if isinstance(row, dict)),\n"
    "            key=lambda row: float(row.get(\"updated_ts\") or 0),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        for row in rows:\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\") != \"completed\" or str(row.get(\"share_id\") or \"\") != share_id:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            path = str(row.get(\"path\") or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "            if not path or explicit_seasons_v1111([path]):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(row.get(\"subscribe_id\"))\n"
    "            if sid <= 0:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                owner = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                owner = None\n"
    "            if not owner or self._xunlei_series_key_v11210(owner) != series:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            return self._xunlei_write_success_claim_v11210(owner, share_id, path)\n"
    "        return {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _xunlei_existing_claim_v11210(self, subscribe: Any, share_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        key = self._xunlei_claim_key_v11210(subscribe, share_id)\n"
    "        if not key or key.endswith(\"|\"):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        with self._xunlei_claim_guard_v11210:\n"
    "            store = self._xunlei_claim_store_v11210()\n"
    "            row = dict((store.get(\"items\") or {}).get(key) or {})\n"
    "        return row or self._xunlei_recover_legacy_claim_v11210(subscribe, share_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # final Xunlei identity gate\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _xunlei_json_identity_matches_v1123(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        candidate: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        info: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        template: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n"
    "        accepted, reason = super()._xunlei_json_identity_matches_v1123(subscribe, candidate, info, template)\n"
    "        if not accepted or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        season = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0))\n"
    "        if season <= 0:\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "        explicit_seasons = self._xunlei_package_explicit_seasons_v11210(template)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 显式季号由既有媒体身份 + planner 负责；真实多季包也不能被 seasonless claim 锁死。\n"
    "        if explicit_seasons:\n"
    "            if len(explicit_seasons) == 1 and season not in explicit_seasons:\n"
    "                return False, f\"迅雷资源明确季号冲突：订阅=S{season:02d} 实际={sorted(explicit_seasons)}\"\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        total = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"total_episode\", 0))\n"
    "        package_episodes = self._xunlei_package_episodes_v11210(subscribe, template)\n"
    "        if total > 0 and package_episodes and max(package_episodes) > total:\n"
    "            actual_max = max(package_episodes)\n"
    "            message = (\n"
    "                f\"迅雷无季号资源结构冲突：订阅 S{season:02d} 共{total}集，\"\n"
    "                f\"但实际包解析到 E{actual_max:02d}；禁止裁剪 E01-E{total:02d} 后把其它季资源冒充本季\"\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷季栅栏v1.12.10】#%s %s\",\n"
    "                self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0)),\n"
    "                message,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return False, message\n"
    "\n"
    "        share_id = str(\n"
    "            candidate.get(\"share_id\")\n"
    "            or candidate.get(\"shareId\")\n"
    "            or template.get(\"shareId\")\n"
    "            or info.get(\"share_id\")\n"
    "            or info.get(\"shareId\")\n"
    "            or \"\"\n"
    "        ).strip()\n"
    "        if not share_id:\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "        claim = self._xunlei_existing_claim_v11210(subscribe, share_id)\n"
    "        if not claim:\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        sid = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0))\n"
    "        owner_sid = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(claim.get(\"subscribe_id\"))\n"
    "        owner_season = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(claim.get(\"season\"))\n"
    "        if owner_sid == sid and sid > 0:\n"
    "            return bool(accepted), str(reason or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        message = (\n"
    "            f\"同一无季号迅雷分享已由本系列 S{owner_season:02d} / 订阅#{owner_sid or '-'} 成功消费；\"\n"
    "            f\"当前 S{season:02d} / 订阅#{sid or '-'} 禁止再次导入同一物理 share\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【迅雷季栅栏v1.12.10】share=%s %s\",\n"
    "            share_id[:24],\n"
    "            message,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return False, message\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # success claim + concurrent series serialization\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _save_xunlei_state(self, state: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        super()._save_xunlei_state(state)\n"
    "        items = state.get(\"items\") if isinstance(state, dict) else None\n"
    "        if not isinstance(items, dict) or not items:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        completed = [\n"
    "            row for row in items.values()\n"
    "            if isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "            and str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"completed\"\n"
    "            and _is_video(str(row.get(\"path\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        if not completed:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        latest = max(float(row.get(\"updated_ts\") or 0) for row in completed)\n"
    "        for row in completed:\n"
    "            if float(row.get(\"updated_ts\") or 0) < latest - 0.01:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = self._safe_positive_int_v11210(row.get(\"subscribe_id\"))\n"
    "            share_id = str(row.get(\"share_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            path = str(row.get(\"path\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if sid <= 0 or not share_id or not path:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                subscribe = None\n"
    "            if subscribe:\n"
    "                self._xunlei_write_success_claim_v11210(subscribe, share_id, path)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "        lock = self._xunlei_series_dispatch_lock_v11210(subscribe)\n"
    "        if lock is None:\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaXunleiSeasonFenceV11210Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== episode_fence_final_v1124.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["episode_fence_final_v1124"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.24 跨来源集级终止栅栏最终补丁。\n"
    "\n"
    "补齐并发/在途边界：\n"
    "- 迅雷秒传、光鸭分享、Magnet、ED2K 不只共享“已成功集”，也共享“正在处理集”；\n"
    "- 外部云添加任务在提交/轮询前再次按实时缺集裁剪；部分重叠任务立即取消旧 task，\n"
    "  只保留真正缺失的集数重新提交；\n"
    "- 每个外部 worker 用线程局部 source_id 排除自身 reservation，避免自己的 dispatching\n"
    "  状态反过来把自己判成重复；\n"
    "- 修正旧栅栏在取消并重建后仍轮询旧 task_id 的边界。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "from .episode_fence_v1124 import GuangYaEpisodeFenceV1124Mixin\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_EXTERNAL_INFLIGHT_V1124 = {\"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\"}\n"
    "_EXTERNAL_ACTIVE_V1124 = {\"new\", \"retry\", \"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaEpisodeFenceFinalV1124Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最终运行时门禁：任何来源成功/在途后，其目标集不允许其它来源重复提交。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260903-r40\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._episode_fence_context_v1124 = threading.local()\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _episode_fence_current_source_v1124(self) -> str:\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_episode_fence_context_v1124\", None)\n"
    "        return str(getattr(context, \"source_id\", \"\") or \"\") if context is not None else \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _pending_reservations(self, subscribe: Any, exclude_job_key: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把 Magnet/ED2K 已提交任务也加入统一 reservation，且排除当前 worker 自己。\"\"\"\n"
    "        base = dict(super()._pending_reservations(subscribe, exclude_job_key=exclude_job_key) or {})\n"
    "        base[\"episodes\"] = set(base.get(\"episodes\") or set())\n"
    "        base[\"paths\"] = set(base.get(\"paths\") or set())\n"
    "\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        current_source_id = self._episode_fence_current_source_v1124()\n"
    "        store_method = getattr(self, \"_source_store\", None)\n"
    "        if sid <= 0 or not callable(store_method):\n"
    "            return base\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            items = (store_method().get(\"items\") or {}).values()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return base\n"
    "\n"
    "        active_episode_claims: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        active_movie = False\n"
    "        for source in items:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(source, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if source_id and source_id == current_source_id:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if int(source.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if not bool(source.get(\"enabled\", True)):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(source.get(\"state\") or \"\") not in _EXTERNAL_INFLIGHT_V1124:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                active_movie = True\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            active_episode_claims.update(self._source_episode_targets_v1124(source))\n"
    "\n"
    "        if active_episode_claims:\n"
    "            base[\"episodes\"].update(active_episode_claims)\n"
    "        if active_movie:\n"
    "            base[\"movie\"] = True\n"
    "        return base\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _mark_uncancellable_source_v1124(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        acquired: Set[int],\n"
    "        reason: str,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"服务端取消失败时本地永久停止重试；绝不再创建第二个同集任务。\"\"\"\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        updated = self._update_source(\n"
    "            source_id,\n"
    "            state=\"disabled\",\n"
    "            enabled=False,\n"
    "            auto_dispatch=False,\n"
    "            superseded_by_receipt=True,\n"
    "            superseded_episodes=sorted(acquired),\n"

    "            remote_cancelled=False,\n"
    "            next_retry_at=0,\n"
    "        ) or source\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"WARNING\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【集级终止】来源 %s 的旧服务端任务无法确认取消；本地已永久停止该来源，绝不再次提交同集任务：%s\",\n"
    "            source_id or \"-\",\n"
    "            reason,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return updated\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _prepare_offline_source_v1124(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        source: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any], str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Magnet/ED2K create/retry/poll 前的最后实时缺集裁剪。\"\"\"\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        targets = self._source_episode_targets_v1124(source)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if self._movie_transfer_confirmed(subscribe):\n"
    "                    updated = self._disable_offline_source_v1124(\n"
    "                        source,\n"
    "                        set(),\n"
    "                        reason=\"电影正片已经成功获得\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return False, updated, \"电影已完成，停止重复云添加\"\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "            return True, source, \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not targets:\n"
    "            return True, source, \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        missing = set(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe))\n"
    "        remaining = targets.intersection(missing)\n"
    "        superseded = targets - remaining\n"
    "        if not remaining:\n"
    "            updated = self._disable_offline_source_v1124(\n"
    "                source,\n"
    "                self._acquired_episode_facts_v1124(subscribe),\n"
    "                reason=\"该来源目标集均已成功获得\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return False, updated, \"目标集已完成，停止重复云添加\"\n"
    "        if remaining == targets:\n"
    "            return True, source, \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        state = str(source.get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "        task_id = str(source.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if task_id:\n"
    "            # fileIndexes 不能在既有 cloudcollection task 上安全原地修改：必须先取消旧 task。\n"
    "            if not self._delete_offline_task_v1124(source):\n"
    "                updated = self._mark_uncancellable_source_v1124(\n"
    "                    source,\n"
    "                    superseded,\n"
    "                    \"旧任务同时包含已完成集和未完成集，取消失败；为避免重复入库停止该来源\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return False, updated, \"旧任务无法安全裁剪，已停止以避免重复\"\n"
    "            updated = self._update_source(\n"
    "                source_id,\n"
    "                state=\"new\",\n"
    "                task_id=\"\",\n"
    "                task_status=0,\n"
    "                progress=0,\n"
    "                target_episodes=sorted(remaining),\n"
    "                resolved_episodes=[],\n"
    "                selected_indexes=[],\n"
    "                resolved_url=\"\",\n"
    "                next_retry_at=0,\n"
    "                superseded_episodes=sorted(superseded),\n"
    "                superseded_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "                remote_cancelled=True,\n"
    "            ) or source\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【集级终止】来源 %s 旧 task=%s 已取消；已完成=%s，仅重建剩余=%s\",\n"
    "                source_id or \"-\",\n"
    "                task_id,\n"
    "                \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in sorted(superseded)),\n"
    "                \",\".join(f\"E{value:02d}\" for value in sorted(remaining)),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return True, updated, \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 尚未生成服务端 task，可以直接把计划缩成剩余集；同时清除旧 resolve 结果，强制重新选 fileIndexes。\n"
    "        next_state = \"new\" if state in {\"retry\", \"dispatching\"} else state or \"new\"\n"
    "        updated = self._update_source(\n"
    "            source_id,\n"
    "            state=next_state,\n"
    "            target_episodes=sorted(remaining),\n"
    "            resolved_episodes=[],\n"
    "            selected_indexes=[],\n"
    "            resolved_url=\"\",\n"
    "            next_retry_at=0,\n"
    "            superseded_episodes=sorted(superseded),\n"
    "            superseded_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "        ) or source\n"
    "        return True, updated, \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _supersede_offline_sources_v1124(self, subscribe: Any, acquired: Set[int]) -> int:\n"
    "        \"\"\"成功回执到达时立即停止/裁剪其它 Magnet/ED2K，而不是等下一轮轮询。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not acquired:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        store_method = getattr(self, \"_source_store\", None)\n"
    "        if sid <= 0 or not callable(store_method):\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            items = list((store_method().get(\"items\") or {}).values())\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "\n"
    "        affected = 0\n"
    "        resume_ids: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for source in items:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(source, dict) or int(source.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            state = str(source.get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if state not in _EXTERNAL_ACTIVE_V1124:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            targets = self._source_episode_targets_v1124(source)\n"
    "            overlap = targets.intersection(acquired)\n"
    "            if not overlap:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            before_task = str(source.get(\"task_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            before_targets = set(targets)\n"
    "            allowed, updated, _ = self._prepare_offline_source_v1124(source, subscribe)\n"
    "            affected += 1\n"
    "            if not allowed:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            after_targets = self._source_episode_targets_v1124(updated)\n"
    "            after_task = str(updated.get(\"task_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            changed_plan = after_targets != before_targets or after_task != before_task\n"
    "            if (\n"
    "                changed_plan\n"
    "                and str(updated.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"new\"\n"
    "                and bool(updated.get(\"enabled\", True))\n"
    "                and bool(updated.get(\"auto_dispatch\", True))\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                source_id = str(updated.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "                if source_id:\n"
    "                    resume_ids.append(source_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # worker 会在当前媒体 RLock 释放后才真正提交，因此一定能读到刚写入的最新缺集事实。\n"
    "        for source_id in dict.fromkeys(resume_ids):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                self._spawn_source_dispatch(source_id)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【集级终止】剩余集任务 %s 自动续跑失败，等待下一轮：%s\",\n"
    "                    source_id,\n"
    "                    str(err)[:260],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        return affected\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _submit_offline_source(self, source_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"记录当前 worker 身份，让统一 reservation 只排除自己、不排除其它在途来源。\"\"\"\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_episode_fence_context_v1124\", None)\n"
    "        if context is None:\n"
    "            self._episode_fence_context_v1124 = threading.local()\n"
    "            context = self._episode_fence_context_v1124\n"
    "        previous = str(getattr(context, \"source_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        context.source_id = str(source_id or \"\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._submit_offline_source(source_id)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            context.source_id = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _poll_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"轮询前实时裁剪，并确保取消重建后绝不再拿旧 task_id 去 poll。\"\"\"\n"
    "        source_id = str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        latest = dict((self._source_store().get(\"items\") or {}).get(source_id) or source)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(int(latest.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0))\n"
    "        if not subscribe:\n"
    "            return super()._poll_offline_source(latest)\n"
    "\n"
    "        context = getattr(self, \"_episode_fence_context_v1124\", None)\n"
    "        if context is None:\n"
    "            self._episode_fence_context_v1124 = threading.local()\n"
    "            context = self._episode_fence_context_v1124\n"
    "        previous = str(getattr(context, \"source_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        context.source_id = source_id\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            lock = self._episode_fence_lock_v1124(subscribe)\n"
    "            with lock:\n"
    "                latest = dict((self._source_store().get(\"items\") or {}).get(source_id) or latest)\n"
    "                fresh = self._find_subscription(int(latest.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)) or subscribe\n"
    "                allowed, prepared, message = self._prepare_offline_source_v1124(latest, fresh)\n"
    "                if not allowed:\n"
    "                    return {\"success\": True, \"handled\": True, \"message\": message, \"data\": prepared}\n"
    "                if not str(prepared.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                    # 旧 task 已因部分重叠被取消；不要 poll 旧 ID。退出本次 poll，由新 worker/下一轮按剩余集 create。\n"
    "                    if str(prepared.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"new\" and bool(prepared.get(\"auto_dispatch\", True)):\n"
    "                        self._spawn_source_dispatch(source_id)\n"
    "                    return {\n"
    "                        \"success\": True,\n"
    "                        \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                        \"message\": \"旧重复任务已终止，仅剩余缺集待重新提交\",\n"
    "                        \"data\": prepared,\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                # 跳过 episode_fence_v1124 的旧 poll 包装，直接进入其后的正常完成回执链。\n"
    "                return super(GuangYaEpisodeFenceV1124Mixin, self)._poll_offline_source(prepared)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            context.source_id = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_xunlei_flash(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"电影若已有其它来源在途则不再抢跑第二份正片；TV 的在途集由 planner reservation 处理。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            pending = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            if bool(pending.get(\"movie\")):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    completed = bool(self._movie_transfer_confirmed(subscribe))\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    completed = False\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": completed,\n"
    "                    \"handled\": completed,\n"
    "                    \"shares\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"attempted_files\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"successful_files\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "                    \"movie\": completed,\n"
    "                    \"message\": \"电影已有成功/在途获取任务，跳过重复迅雷秒传\" if not completed else \"电影已成功获得，跳过重复迅雷秒传\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        return super()._dispatch_xunlei_flash(subscribe)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaEpisodeFenceFinalV1124Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

    "    def _save_xunlei_state(self, state: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"

    "_GYING_URL_RE = re.compile(r\"https?://[^\\s\\\"'<>，。；;]+\", re.I)\n"
    "_XUNLEI_URL_RE = re.compile(r\"https?://pan\\.xunlei\\.com/s/[^\\s\\\"'<>，。；;]+\", re.I)\n"
    "_PASSCODE_RE = re.compile(r\"(?:提取码|访问码|密码|口令|pass\\s*code|passcode|pwd)\\s*[:：=]?\\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{1,16})\", re.I)\n"
    "_CHALLENGE_MARKERS = (\n"
    "    \"正在确认你是不是机器人\",\n"
    "    \"浏览器安全验证\",\n"
    "    \"正在进行浏览器计算验证\",\n"
    "    \"安全验证\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_LOGIN_MARKERS = (\n"
    "    \"_BT.PC.HTML('login')\",\n"
    "    '_BT.PC.HTML(\"login\")',\n"
    "    \"_BT.PC.HTML('nologin')\",\n"
    "    '_BT.PC.HTML(\"nologin\")',\n"
    "    \"未登录，访问受限\",\n"
    ")\n"
    "_MAINTENANCE_MARKERS = (\"站点维护中\", \"该站点维护中\", \"站点正在维护\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _safe_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        return int(value or 0)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return default\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _normalize_node_url(value: str) -> str:\n"
    "    raw = html.unescape(str(value or \"\").strip()).strip(\"`\\\"'()[]{}，。；;\")\n"
    "    if not raw:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if not raw.lower().startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n"
    "        raw = \"https://\" + raw\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        parsed = urlparse(raw)\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if parsed.scheme not in {\"http\", \"https\"} or not parsed.hostname:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    if parsed.username or parsed.password:\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    host = parsed.hostname.strip(\".\")\n"
    "    if host.lower() in {\"localhost\", \"localhost.localdomain\"} or host.startswith(\"127.\") or host.endswith(\".local\"):\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "    netloc = parsed.netloc\n"
    "    return urlunparse((parsed.scheme, netloc, \"\", \"\", \"\", \"\")).rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _registry_node_candidate(value: str) -> bool:\n"
    "    url = _normalize_node_url(value)\n"
    "    if not url:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    host = str(urlparse(url).hostname or \"\")\n"
    "    lowered = host.lower()\n"
    "    if any(token in lowered for token in (\"gying\", \"gyg\")) or lowered.startswith(\"xn--\"):\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    return any(ord(ch) > 127 for ch in host)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _cookie_header(session: requests.Session) -> str:\n"
    "    pairs = []\n"
    "    for item in session.cookies:\n"
    "        if item.name and item.value:\n"
    "            pairs.append(f\"{item.name}={item.value}\")\n"
    "    return \"; \".join(pairs)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _apply_cookie_header(session: requests.Session, value: str) -> None:\n"
    "    raw = str(value or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not raw:\n"
    "        return\n"
    "    cookie = SimpleCookie()\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        cookie.load(raw)\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        cookie = SimpleCookie()\n"
    "    if cookie:\n"
    "        for key, morsel in cookie.items():\n"
    "            session.cookies.set(str(key), str(morsel.value))\n"
    "        return\n"
    "    for part in raw.split(\";\"):\n"
    "        key, sep, val = part.strip().partition(\"=\")\n"
    "        if sep and key:\n"
    "            session.cookies.set(key.strip(), val.strip())\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _is_challenge_text(text: str) -> bool:\n"
    "    body = str(text or \"\")\n"
    "    return any(marker in body for marker in _CHALLENGE_MARKERS)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _solve_pow_hex(modulus_hex: str, value_hex: str, rounds: int) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"复现 GYING worker：y=(y*y)%N，返回不带 0x 的十六进制 y。\"\"\"\n"
    "    rounds = _safe_int(rounds, 0)\n"
    "    if rounds <= 0 or rounds > 2_000_000:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING PoW 迭代次数异常\")\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        modulus = int(str(modulus_hex or \"\").removeprefix(\"0x\"), 16)\n"
    "        value = int(str(value_hex or \"\").removeprefix(\"0x\"), 16)\n"
    "    except Exception as err:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING PoW 参数无效\") from err\n"
    "    if modulus <= 1:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING PoW 模数无效\")\n"
    "    for _ in range(rounds):\n"
    "        value = (value * value) % modulus\n"
    "    return format(value, \"x\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _solve_legacy_nonces(challenges: Iterable[str], salt: str, diff: int) -> List[int]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"兼容旧版 challenge/diff/salt：sha256(str(nonce)+salt)。\"\"\"\n"
    "    wanted = [str(item or \"\").strip().lower() for item in challenges if str(item or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "    if not wanted:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING legacy challenge 为空\")\n"
    "    limit = _safe_int(diff, 0)\n"
    "    if limit <= 0 or limit > 5_000_000:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING legacy challenge diff 异常\")\n"
    "    remaining = set(wanted)\n"
    "    found: Dict[str, int] = {}\n"
    "    for nonce in range(limit + 1):\n"
    "        digest = hashlib.sha256(f\"{nonce}{salt}\".encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest().lower()\n"
    "        if digest in remaining:\n"
    "            found[digest] = nonce\n"
    "            remaining.discard(digest)\n"
    "            if not remaining:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "    if remaining:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"GYING legacy challenge 未找到全部 nonce\")\n"
    "    return [found[item] for item in wanted]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _parse_search_payload_result_v11223(text: str) -> Tuple[bool, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"区分“搜索结果确实为空”和“页面结构无法解析”。\n"
    "\n"
    "    GYING 的新版搜索页会用合法的 ``_obj.search`` 对象表达零结果。旧调用方只看\n"
    "    空列表，因而把合法零结果误当作协议不兼容并切到 legacy 搜索；部分节点的 legacy\n"
    "    页面会返回默认推荐卡片。布尔值只描述响应能否被解析，不描述目标是否命中。\n"
    "    \"\"\"\n"
    "    match = _GYING_SEARCH_RE.search(str(text or \"\"))\n"
    "    if not match:\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        payload = json.loads(match.group(1))\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    listing = payload.get(\"l\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n"
    "    if not isinstance(listing, dict):\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    core_values = (listing.get(\"title\"), listing.get(\"d\"), listing.get(\"i\"))\n"
    "    if not all(isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) for value in core_values):\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    titles, kinds, ids = (list(value) for value in core_values)\n"
    "    if len({len(titles), len(kinds), len(ids)}) != 1:\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    years_value = listing.get(\"year\")\n"
    "    infos_value = listing.get(\"info\")\n"
    "    years = list(years_value) if isinstance(years_value, (list, tuple)) else []\n"
    "    infos = list(infos_value) if isinstance(infos_value, (list, tuple)) else []\n"
    "    size = len(titles)\n"
    "    declared_count = payload.get(\"n\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        if size == 0 and declared_count not in (None, \"\") and int(declared_count) > 0:\n"
    "            return False, []\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        pass\n"
    "    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "    for index in range(size):\n"
    "        title = str(titles[index] if index < len(titles) else \"\").strip()\n"
    "        kind = str(kinds[index] if index < len(kinds) else \"\").strip()\n"
    "        resource_id = str(ids[index] if index < len(ids) else \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not title or not kind or not resource_id:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        rows.append({\n"
    "            \"title\": title,\n"
    "            \"year\": years[index] if index < len(years) else \"\",\n"
    "            \"type\": kind,\n"
    "            \"id\": resource_id,\n"
    "            \"info\": str(infos[index] if index < len(infos) else \"\").strip(),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "    # 非空核心数组中出现空标题、空类型或空 ID 属于结构损坏，不能伪装成合法零结果。\n"
    "    if size and len(rows) != size:\n"
    "        return False, []\n"
    "    return True, rows\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _parse_search_payload(text: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"兼容旧调用方；新搜索链使用带解析状态的 v1.12.23 helper。\"\"\"\n"
    "    return _parse_search_payload_result_v11223(text)[1]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _extract_panlist(payload: Any) -> Dict[str, List[Any]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"不同节点偶尔把 panlist 放在顶层或 data/downlist 内，递归找到 name/url 数组。\"\"\"\n"
    "    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n"
    "        panlist = payload.get(\"panlist\")\n"
    "        if isinstance(panlist, dict) and isinstance(panlist.get(\"url\"), list):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"url\": list(panlist.get(\"url\") or []),\n"
    "                \"name\": list(panlist.get(\"name\") or []),\n"
    "                \"type\": list(panlist.get(\"type\") or []),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        for value in payload.values():\n"
    "            found = _extract_panlist(value)\n"
    "            if found.get(\"url\"):\n"
    "                return found\n"
    "    elif isinstance(payload, (list, tuple)):\n"
    "        for value in payload:\n"
    "            found = _extract_panlist(value)\n"
    "            if found.get(\"url\"):\n"
    "                return found\n"
    "    return {\"url\": [], \"name\": [], \"type\": []}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaGyingRuntimeMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"观影节点选择、challenge、登录、搜索与会话持久化最终实现。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    _viewing_registry_urls = DEFAULT_GYING_REGISTRIES\n"
    "    _viewing_node_urls = \"\"\n"
    "    _viewing_auto_switch = True\n"
    "    _viewing_auto_challenge = True\n"
    "    _viewing_node_cache_minutes = 360\n"
    "    _gying_active_node = \"\"\n"
    "    _gying_search_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        self._viewing_registry_urls = str(config.get(\"viewing_registry_urls\") or DEFAULT_GYING_REGISTRIES).strip()\n"
    "        self._viewing_node_urls = str(config.get(\"viewing_node_urls\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        self._viewing_auto_switch = bool(config.get(\"viewing_auto_switch\", True))\n"
    "        self._viewing_auto_challenge = bool(config.get(\"viewing_auto_challenge\", True))\n"
    "        self._viewing_node_cache_minutes = max(10, min(_safe_int(config.get(\"viewing_node_cache_minutes\"), 360), 1440))\n"
    "        self._gying_active_node = \"\"\n"
    "        self._gying_search_cache = {}\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 私有状态 / 节点池\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"viewing_session_state\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        state.setdefault(\"schema\", 2)\n"
    "        state.setdefault(\"active_node\", \"\")\n"
    "        state.setdefault(\"discovered_at\", 0)\n"
    "        state.setdefault(\"nodes\", {})\n"
    "        return state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _save_gying_state(self, state: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        nodes = state.get(\"nodes\") or {}\n"
    "        if isinstance(nodes, dict) and len(nodes) > 40:\n"
    "            ordered = sorted(\n"
    "                nodes.items(),\n"
    "                key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"last_ok_ts\") or (pair[1] or {}).get(\"last_checked_ts\") or 0),\n"
    "                reverse=True,\n"
    "            )[:40]\n"
    "            state[\"nodes\"] = dict(ordered)\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"viewing_session_state\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_new_session(self, node: str, saved_cookie: str = \"\") -> requests.Session:\n"
    "        session = requests.Session()\n"
    "        session.headers.update({\n"
    "            \"User-Agent\": \"Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/140.0.0.0 Safari/537.36\",\n"
    "            \"Accept\": \"text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/json;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8\",\n"
    "            \"Accept-Language\": \"zh-CN,zh;q=0.9,en;q=0.7\",\n"
    "            \"Cache-Control\": \"no-cache\",\n"
    "            \"Pragma\": \"no-cache\",\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua\": '\"Chromium\";v=\"140\", \"Google Chrome\";v=\"140\", \"Not_A Brand\";v=\"99\"',\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua-mobile\": \"?0\",\n"
    "            \"sec-ch-ua-platform\": '\"Windows\"',\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        proxies = _proxy_dict(bool(getattr(self, \"_provider_proxy\", False)))\n"
    "        if proxies:\n"
    "            session.proxies.update(proxies)\n"
    "        if saved_cookie:\n"
    "            _apply_cookie_header(session, saved_cookie)\n"
    "        if str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_cookie\", \"\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            _apply_cookie_header(session, str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_cookie\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        return session\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _discover_gying_nodes(self, force: bool = False) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        cached = list(state.get(\"discovered_nodes\") or [])\n"
    "        ttl = float(self._viewing_node_cache_minutes) * 60.0\n"
    "        if cached and not force and now - float(state.get(\"discovered_at\") or 0) < ttl:\n"
    "            return cached\n"
    "\n"
    "        rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "        preferred = _normalize_node_url(str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        if preferred:\n"
    "            rows.append(preferred)\n"
    "        for raw in self._viewing_node_urls.splitlines():\n"
    "            node = _normalize_node_url(raw)\n"
    "            if node:\n"
    "                rows.append(node)\n"
    "\n"
    "        session = self._gying_new_session(preferred or \"\")\n"
    "        for raw in self._viewing_registry_urls.splitlines():\n"
    "            registry = _normalize_node_url(raw)\n"
    "            if not registry:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                response = session.get(registry + \"/\", timeout=min(int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15), 20), allow_redirects=True)\n"
    "                text = html.unescape(str(response.text or \"\"))\n"
    "                values = list(_GYING_URL_RE.findall(text))\n"
    "                values.extend(str(match) for match in re.findall(r\"href=[\\\"']([^\\\"']+)[\\\"']\", text, flags=re.I))\n"
    "                for value in values:\n"
    "                    absolute = urljoin(response.url, value)\n"
    "                    if _registry_node_candidate(absolute):\n"
    "                        rows.append(_normalize_node_url(absolute))\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "\n"
    "        rows.extend(DEFAULT_GYING_SEEDS)\n"
    "        dedup: List[str] = []\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        for value in rows:\n"
    "            node = _normalize_node_url(value)\n"
    "            if not node or node in seen:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            seen.add(node)\n"
    "            dedup.append(node)\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_nodes\"] = dedup[:30]\n"
    "        state[\"discovered_at\"] = now\n"
    "        self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "        return dedup[:30]\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Challenge / 会话\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_solve_challenge(self, session: requests.Session, node: str, response: requests.Response) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if not self._viewing_auto_challenge:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影返回浏览器安全验证；自动计算验证已关闭\")\n"
    "        text = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "        match = _GYING_CHALLENGE_RE.search(text)\n"
    "        if match:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                data = json.loads(match.group(1))\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影验证数据解析失败\") from err\n"
    "            started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "            challenge_id = str(data.get(\"id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if data.get(\"N\") and data.get(\"x\") and data.get(\"t\"):\n"
    "                y = _solve_pow_hex(str(data.get(\"N\")), str(data.get(\"x\")), _safe_int(data.get(\"t\"), 0))\n"
    "                elapsed = time.monotonic() - started\n"
    "                if elapsed < 3.0:\n"
    "                    time.sleep(3.0 - elapsed)\n"
    "                verify = session.post(\n"
    "                    response.url,\n"
    "                    data={\"action\": \"verify\", \"id\": challenge_id, \"y\": y},\n"
    "                    headers={\"Referer\": response.url},\n"
    "                    timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15) + 15,\n"
    "                    allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if verify.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(f\"观影 PoW 提交失败 HTTP {verify.status_code}\")\n"
    "                return {\"mode\": \"embedded_pow\", \"success\": True}\n"
    "            challenges = data.get(\"challenge\") or []\n"
    "            if challenges and data.get(\"diff\") and data.get(\"salt\") is not None:\n"
    "                nonces = _solve_legacy_nonces(challenges, str(data.get(\"salt\") or \"\"), _safe_int(data.get(\"diff\"), 0))\n"
    "                form: List[Tuple[str, str]] = [(\"action\", \"verify\"), (\"id\", challenge_id)]\n"
    "                form.extend((\"nonce[]\", str(value)) for value in nonces)\n"
    "                verify = session.post(\n"
    "                    response.url,\n"
    "                    data=form,\n"
    "                    headers={\"Referer\": response.url},\n"
    "                    timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15) + 15,\n"
    "                    allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if verify.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(f\"观影 legacy 验证提交失败 HTTP {verify.status_code}\")\n"
    "                return {\"mode\": \"legacy_hash\", \"success\": True}\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 新版远程 PoW：challenge 页通过 browser_pow 标记，真实 N/x/t 在 /res/pow。\n"
    "        pow_url = node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/res/pow\"\n"
    "        started = time.monotonic()\n"
    "        pow_resp = session.get(pow_url, headers={\"Referer\": response.url}, timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15))\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            data = pow_resp.json()\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影远程 PoW 参数获取失败\") from err\n"
    "        if not isinstance(data, dict) or not data.get(\"N\") or not data.get(\"x\") or not data.get(\"t\"):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影远程 PoW 参数不完整\")\n"
    "        y = _solve_pow_hex(str(data.get(\"N\")), str(data.get(\"x\")), _safe_int(data.get(\"t\"), 0))\n"
    "        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started\n"
    "        if elapsed < 3.0:\n"
    "            time.sleep(3.0 - elapsed)\n"
    "        verify = session.post(\n"
    "            pow_url,\n"
    "            data={\"y\": y},\n"
    "            headers={\"Referer\": response.url},\n"
    "            timeout=int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15) + 15,\n"
    "            allow_redirects=True,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = verify.json()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            payload = {}\n"
    "        if verify.status_code >= 400 or (isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and payload.get(\"success\") is False):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影远程 PoW 提交失败 HTTP {verify.status_code}\")\n"
    "        return {\"mode\": \"remote_pow\", \"success\": True, \"challenge_id\": str((payload or {}).get(\"challenge_id\") or \"\")}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_request(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        session: requests.Session,\n"
    "        node: str,\n"
    "        method: str,\n"
    "        url: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        retry_challenge: bool = True,\n"
    "        **kwargs: Any,\n"
    "    ) -> requests.Response:\n"
    "        timeout = kwargs.pop(\"timeout\", int(getattr(self, \"_provider_timeout\", 15) or 15))\n"
    "        response = session.request(method.upper(), url, timeout=timeout, allow_redirects=True, **kwargs)\n"
    "        if retry_challenge and _is_challenge_text(response.text or \"\"):\n"
    "            self._gying_solve_challenge(session, node, response)\n"
    "            response = session.request(method.upper(), url, timeout=timeout, allow_redirects=True, **kwargs)\n"
    "            if _is_challenge_text(response.text or \"\"):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影浏览器验证已计算，但原请求仍返回验证页；请尝试更换节点/代理\")\n"
    "        return response\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _gying_login_required(response: requests.Response) -> bool:\n"
    "        text = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "        return response.status_code == 403 or any(marker in text for marker in _LOGIN_MARKERS)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_persist_session(self, node: str, session: requests.Session, **extra: Any) -> None:\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        nodes = state.setdefault(\"nodes\", {})\n"
    "        row = dict(nodes.get(node) or {})\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        row.update({\n"
    "            \"status\": str(extra.pop(\"status\", \"ok\")),\n"
    "            \"cookie\": _cookie_header(session),\n"
    "            \"last_checked_ts\": now,\n"
    "            \"last_ok_ts\": now,\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "            **extra,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        nodes[node] = row\n"
    "        state[\"active_node\"] = node\n"
    "        self._gying_active_node = node\n"
    "        self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_mark_node(self, node: str, status: str, message: str = \"\") -> None:\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        nodes = state.setdefault(\"nodes\", {})\n"
    "        row = dict(nodes.get(node) or {})\n"
    "        row.update({\n"
    "            \"status\": status,\n"
    "            \"message\": str(message or \"\")[:300],\n"
    "            \"last_checked_ts\": time.time(),\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        nodes[node] = row\n"
    "        self._save_gying_state(state)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_login(self, session: requests.Session, node: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        username = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_username\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        password = str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_password\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if not (username and password):\n"
    "            mode = \"cookie\" if len(session.cookies) else \"anonymous\"\n"
    "            return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": mode, \"message\": \"复用现有观影 Cookie\" if mode == \"cookie\" else \"未配置观影账号\"}\n"
    "        payload = {\n"
    "            \"code\": \"\",\n"
    "            \"siteid\": \"1\",\n"
    "            \"dosubmit\": \"1\",\n"
    "            \"cookietime\": \"10506240\",\n"
    "            \"username\": username,\n"
    "            \"password\": password,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        login_url = node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/user/login\"\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(\n"
    "            session,\n"
    "            node,\n"
    "            \"POST\",\n"
    "            login_url,\n"
    "            data=payload,\n"
    "            headers={\"Referer\": node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\"},\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = response.json()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            result = {}\n"
    "        code = (result or {}).get(\"code\") if isinstance(result, dict) else None\n"
    "        if code not in (200, \"200\"):\n"
    "            message = str((result or {}).get(\"msg\") or (result or {}).get(\"message\") or response.text or f\"HTTP {response.status_code}\")[:300]\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"mode\": \"password\", \"message\": f\"观影登录失败：{message}\", \"status\": response.status_code}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/mv/wkMn\")\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        self._gying_persist_session(node, session, status=\"ok\", login_mode=\"password\", login_at=self._now_text())\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"mode\": \"password\", \"message\": \"观影账号登录成功\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _viewing_session(self) -> Tuple[requests.Session, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        if not bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            return self._gying_new_session(\"\"), {\"success\": False, \"mode\": \"disabled\", \"message\": \"观影未启用\"}\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        nodes = self._discover_gying_nodes(force=False)\n"
    "        active = _normalize_node_url(str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        preferred = _normalize_node_url(str(getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        ordered: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for node in (active, preferred, *nodes):\n"
    "            if node and node not in ordered:\n"
    "                ordered.append(node)\n"
    "        if not bool(self._viewing_auto_switch) and preferred:\n"
    "            ordered = [preferred]\n"
    "        errors: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for node in ordered[:12]:\n"
    "            saved = str(((state.get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(node) or {}).get(\"cookie\") or \"\")\n"
    "            session = self._gying_new_session(node, saved_cookie=saved)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                response = self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", node.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/\")\n"
    "                body = str(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "                if any(marker in body for marker in _MAINTENANCE_MARKERS):\n"
    "                    self._gying_mark_node(node, \"maintenance\", \"站点维护中\")\n"
    "                    errors.append(f\"{node}: 维护中\")\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if response.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                    self._gying_mark_node(node, \"blocked\", f\"HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "                    errors.append(f\"{node}: HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                login = self._gying_login(session, node)\n"
    "                if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                    self._gying_mark_node(node, \"login_failed\", str(login.get(\"message\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                    return session, {\"node\": node, **login}\n"
    "                self._gying_persist_session(\n"
    "                    node,\n"
    "                    session,\n"
    "                    status=\"ok\",\n"
    "                    login_mode=str(login.get(\"mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    verified=bool(session.cookies.get(\"browser_verified\") or session.cookies.get(\"browser_pow\")),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return session, {\"success\": True, \"node\": node, **login}\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._gying_mark_node(node, \"error\", str(err))\n"
    "                errors.append(f\"{node}: {str(err)[:120]}\")\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "        return self._gying_new_session(preferred or \"\"), {\n"
    "            \"success\": False,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"unavailable\",\n"
    "            \"node\": preferred,\n"
    "            \"message\": (\"；\".join(errors[:4]) or \"没有可用观影节点\")[:500],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 搜索 / downurl（Magnet 与迅雷共用一次结果）\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _gying_detail(self, session: requests.Session, node: str, resource_type: str, resource_id: str, referer: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        url = f\"{node.rstrip('/')}/res/downurl/{quote(str(resource_type or '').strip())}/{quote(str(resource_id or '').strip())}\"\n"
    "        response = self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", url, headers={\"Referer\": referer})\n"
    "        if self._gying_login_required(response):\n"
    "            login = self._gying_login(session, node)\n"
    "            if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(str(login.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失效\"))\n"
    "            response = self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", url, headers={\"Referer\": referer})\n"
    "        if response.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(f\"观影 downurl HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            payload = response.json()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                payload = json.loads(response.text or \"{}\")\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影 downurl 返回非 JSON\") from err\n"
    "        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get(\"code\") in (403, \"403\"):\n"
    "            raise RuntimeError(\"观影 downurl 返回未登录\")\n"
    "        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_raw_results(self, keyword: str, force: bool = False) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        keyword = str(keyword or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not keyword:\n"
    "            return [], {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"观影搜索关键词为空\"}\n"
    "        cache_key_getter = getattr(self, \"_gying_search_cache_key_v11223\", None)\n"
    "        cache_key = cache_key_getter(keyword) if callable(cache_key_getter) else keyword\n"
    "        cached = dict(self._gying_search_cache.get(cache_key) or {})\n"
    "        if cached and not force and time.time() - float(cached.get(\"ts\") or 0) < 120:\n"
    "            return list(cached.get(\"rows\") or []), dict(cached.get(\"state\") or {})\n"
    "        session, login = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "        if not login.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing\", **login}\n"
    "        node = str(login.get(\"node\") or self._gying_active_node or getattr(self, \"_viewing_base_url\", \"\") or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            search_url = f\"{node}/search?q={quote(keyword)}&type=0&mode=2\"\n"
    "            response = self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", search_url, headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"})\n"
    "            if self._gying_login_required(response):\n"
    "                relogin = self._gying_login(session, node)\n"
    "                if not relogin.get(\"success\"):\n"
    "                    raise RuntimeError(str(relogin.get(\"message\") or \"观影登录失效\"))\n"
    "                response = self._gying_request(session, node, \"GET\", search_url, headers={\"Referer\": node + \"/\"})\n"
    "            if response.status_code >= 400:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(f\"观影搜索 HTTP {response.status_code}\")\n"
    "            parsed_ok, cards = _parse_search_payload_result_v11223(response.text or \"\")\n"
    "            if not parsed_ok:\n"
    "                raise RuntimeError(\"观影搜索响应缺少有效 _obj.search 数据\")\n"
    "            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "            for item in cards[: int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20)]:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    payload = self._gying_detail(session, node, str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\"), str(item.get(\"id\") or \"\"), response.url)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                panlist = _extract_panlist(payload)\n"
    "                urls = list(panlist.get(\"url\") or [])\n"
    "                names = list(panlist.get(\"name\") or [])\n"
    "                types = list(panlist.get(\"type\") or [])\n"
    "                for index, value in enumerate(urls):\n"
    "                    rows.append({\n"
    "                        \"url\": str(value or \"\"),\n"
    "                        \"name\": str(names[index] if index < len(names) else item.get(\"title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                        \"pan_type\": types[index] if index < len(types) else None,\n"
    "                        \"search_title\": str(item.get(\"title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                        \"year\": item.get(\"year\"),\n"
    "                        \"resource_type\": str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                        \"resource_id\": str(item.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    })\n"
    "            self._gying_persist_session(node, session, status=\"ok\", login_mode=str(login.get(\"mode\") or \"\"), last_search_at=self._now_text())\n"
    "            state = {\n"
    "                \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"node\": node,\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"),\n"
    "                \"cards\": len(cards),\n"
    "                \"resources\": len(rows),\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"观影搜索成功：{len(cards)} 个影视结果，{len(rows)} 条网盘/资源链接\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            self._gying_search_cache[cache_key] = {\"ts\": time.time(), \"rows\": rows, \"state\": state}\n"
    "            return rows, state\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._gying_mark_node(node, \"search_error\", str(err))\n"
    "            return [], {\"provider\": \"viewing\", \"success\": False, \"node\": node, \"login_mode\": login.get(\"mode\"), \"message\": str(err)[:400]}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing(self, keyword: str) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        rows, state = self._gying_raw_results(keyword)\n"
    "        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in rows:\n"
    "            for row in _find_links(str(item.get(\"url\") or \"\"), name=str(item.get(\"name\") or item.get(\"search_title\") or \"\"), provider=\"viewing\"):\n"
    "                row[\"search_title\"] = str(item.get(\"search_title\") or \"\")\n"
    "                candidates.append(row)\n"
    "        candidates = _dedupe_candidates(candidates)[: int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20)]\n"
    "        return candidates, {**state, \"message\": f\"观影搜索成功，得到 {len(candidates)} 个 Magnet/ED2K 候选\" if state.get(\"success\") else state.get(\"message\")}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _search_viewing_xunlei(self, keyword: str) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        rows, state = self._gying_raw_results(keyword)\n"
    "        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in rows:\n"
    "            value = html.unescape(str(item.get(\"url\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            for match in _XUNLEI_URL_RE.finditer(value):\n"
    "                raw_url = match.group(0).rstrip(\").]】）}\")\n"
    "                parsed = urlparse(raw_url)\n"
    "                share_match = re.search(r\"^/s/([^/?#]+)\", parsed.path or \"\", re.I)\n"
    "                if not share_match:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                passcode = \"\"\n"
    "                query = dict((key, values) for key, values in __import__(\"urllib.parse\", fromlist=[\"parse_qs\"]).parse_qs(parsed.query or \"\").items())\n"
    "                for key in (\"pwd\", \"passcode\", \"pass_code\", \"code\"):\n"
    "                    values = query.get(key) or []\n"
    "                    if values and str(values[0] or \"\").strip():\n"
    "                        passcode = str(values[0]).strip()\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "                if not passcode:\n"
    "                    nearby = f\"{item.get('name') or ''} {value}\"\n"
    "                    code_match = _PASSCODE_RE.search(nearby)\n"
    "                    if code_match:\n"
    "                        passcode = code_match.group(1).strip()\n"
    "                candidates.append({\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"xunlei\",\n"
    "                    \"uri\": raw_url,\n"
    "                    \"identity\": share_match.group(1).strip(),\n"
    "                    \"share_id\": share_match.group(1).strip(),\n"
    "                    \"passcode\": passcode,\n"
    "                    \"name\": str(item.get(\"name\") or item.get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                    \"search_title\": str(item.get(\"search_title\") or \"\").strip(),\n"
    "                    \"provider\": \"viewing\",\n"
    "                })\n"
    "        dedup: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for row in candidates:\n"
    "            key = str(row.get(\"share_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            old = dedup.get(key)\n"
    "            if not old or (not old.get(\"passcode\") and row.get(\"passcode\")):\n"
    "                dedup[key] = row\n"
    "        output = list(dedup.values())[: int(getattr(self, \"_provider_result_limit\", 20) or 20)]\n"
    "        return output, {**state, \"provider\": \"viewing_xunlei\", \"message\": f\"观影找到 {len(output)} 个迅雷分享候选\" if state.get(\"success\") else state.get(\"message\")}\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 对外诊断 API（不返回 Cookie/密码）\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def api_viewing_nodes(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        state = self._gying_state()\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        for node in self._discover_gying_nodes(force=False):\n"
    "            saved = dict(((state.get(\"nodes\") or {}).get(node) or {}))\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"url\": node,\n"
    "                \"active\": node == str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"status\": str(saved.get(\"status\") or \"unknown\"),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(saved.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:200],\n"
    "                \"verified\": bool(saved.get(\"verified\")),\n"
    "                \"login_mode\": str(saved.get(\"login_mode\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"updated_at\": str(saved.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"active_node\": str(state.get(\"active_node\") or \"\"), \"data\": rows}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_nodes_refresh(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        nodes = self._discover_gying_nodes(force=True)\n"
    "        session, status = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "        del session\n"
    "        return {\"success\": bool(status.get(\"success\")), \"message\": str(status.get(\"message\") or \"节点刷新完成\"), \"active_node\": str(status.get(\"node\") or \"\"), \"count\": len(nodes), \"data\": self.api_viewing_nodes().get(\"data\")}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_viewing_session_test(self, keyword: str = \"\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        session, status = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "        del session\n"
    "        result: Dict[str, Any] = {\"success\": bool(status.get(\"success\")), \"node\": str(status.get(\"node\") or \"\"), \"mode\": str(status.get(\"mode\") or \"\"), \"message\": str(status.get(\"message\") or \"\")}\n"
    "        if result[\"success\"] and str(keyword or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            rows, search_state = self._gying_raw_results(str(keyword).strip(), force=True)\n"
    "            result.update({\n"
    "                \"success\": bool(search_state.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(search_state.get(\"message\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"resources\": len(rows),\n"
    "                \"cards\": int(search_state.get(\"cards\") or 0),\n"
    "                \"transport\": str(search_state.get(\"transport\") or \"unknown\"),\n"
    "                \"cache_hit\": bool(search_state.get(\"cache_hit\")),\n"
    "                \"search_http_requests\": int(search_state.get(\"search_http_requests\") or 0),\n"
    "                \"downurl_attempts\": int(search_state.get(\"downurl_attempts\") or 0),\n"
    "                \"downurl_success\": int(search_state.get(\"downurl_success\") or 0),\n"
    "                \"downurl_failures\": int(search_state.get(\"downurl_failures\") or 0),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_provider_test(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        base = dict(super().api_provider_test() or {})\n"
    "        providers = [row for row in (base.get(\"data\") or []) if str((row or {}).get(\"provider\") or \"\") != \"viewing\"]\n"
    "        if bool(getattr(self, \"_viewing_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            _, status = self._viewing_session()\n"
    "            providers.insert(0, {\"provider\": \"viewing\", \"success\": bool(status.get(\"success\")), \"node\": str(status.get(\"node\") or \"\"), \"mode\": str(status.get(\"mode\") or \"\"), \"message\": str(status.get(\"message\") or \"\")})\n"
    "        return {\"success\": all(bool(item.get(\"success\")) for item in providers) if providers else True, \"data\": providers}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_api(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        apis = list(super().get_api() or [])\n"
    "        paths = {str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\") for item in apis if isinstance(item, dict)}\n"
    "        extras = [\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/nodes\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_nodes, \"methods\": [\"GET\"], \"summary\": \"查看观影节点池\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/nodes/refresh\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_nodes_refresh, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"刷新观影节点并选择可用节点\"},\n"
    "            {\"path\": \"/viewing/session/test\", \"endpoint\": self.api_viewing_session_test, \"methods\": [\"POST\"], \"summary\": \"测试观影验证/登录/搜索会话\"},\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        apis.extend(item for item in extras if item[\"path\"] not in paths)\n"
    "        return apis\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"GuangYaGyingRuntimeMixin\",\n"
    "    \"_normalize_node_url\",\n"
    "    \"_parse_search_payload\",\n"
    "    \"_parse_search_payload_result_v11223\",\n"
    "    \"_solve_pow_hex\",\n"
    "    \"_solve_legacy_nonces\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["provider_reliability_v1100"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.10.0 外部资源搜索可靠性与统一搜索视图。\n"
    "\n"
    "解决两个长期体验问题：\n"
    "- 通用 Magnet/ED2K 接口不再只假定一个查询参数；按 provider 类型在 q/kw/keyword/search\n"
    "  之间做有限、可诊断的兼容尝试，并统一 Bearer/X-API-Key 认证头；\n"
    "- “观影搜索”不再只展示 Magnet/ED2K。统一搜索同时返回观影迅雷分享、观影 Magnet/ED2K\n"
    "  以及外部 API 候选，让 UI 和自动分流看到同一套真实来源。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.10.3 修复 v1.10.0 重构时的方法名漂移：新版控制台与统一搜索调用\n"
    "``_parse_provider_defs``，而 v1.9.2 的真实配置解析入口仍叫 ``_provider_api_defs``。\n"
    "增加兼容桥接后，状态页、资源来源检测和统一搜索重新使用同一份 Magnet/ED2K 配置定义。\n"
    "\n"
    "v1.12.19 开发阶段增加 Provider 性能、排序与并发状态收口：\n"
    "- GYING 继续单线程执行，避免节点健康/登录会话被并发放大；\n"
    "- 独立 Magnet/ED2K API 单次搜索最多 4 路并发，并使用进程级 4 槽 BoundedSemaphore 约束多个订阅同时搜索的总并发；\n"
    "- executor.map 按配置顺序收敛结果，不让网络返回快慢改变候选先后语义；\n"
    "- URL 关键词模板只请求一次，不再因 q/keyword/kw/search 变体重复请求同一 URL；\n"
    "- 普通 API 记住最近 6 小时真正返回过资源的查询参数，下次优先尝试；缓存命中为空仍继续其它参数，不牺牲召回；\n"
    "- 不再“全局先截断、外层再评分”，而是先汇总单源有界候选池、排序、按 identity 去重，最后截断；\n"
    "- 同一物理资源的重复候选不再默认“配置靠前者获胜”，而是保留排序更优的来源记录；\n"
    "- 自动分流已有订阅上下文时，canonical identity 已明确拒绝的候选在最终 limit 前淘汰，避免错误 Magnet 饿死合法 ED2K；\n"
    "- source store 的 upsert/update/delete 在最终运行时使用同一进程级 RLock，避免多个离线 worker 的 read-modify-write 相互覆盖，也让终态质量学习只观察一次真实迁移；\n"
    "- 来源失败学习采用高置信归责：真实 task 最终 status=5、resolve 后规则不匹配或无媒体文件才扣来源分；订阅删除、网络/API/目标路径等基础设施失败不污染 provider 质量；\n"
    "- 任一 API worker 的意外异常只降级该来源，不允许拖垮其它 Provider。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"

    "_PROVIDER_QUERY_HINT_TTL_V11219 = 6 * 60 * 60\n"

    "_PROVIDER_QUERY_HINT_LOCK_V11219 = threading.RLock()\n"
